import { GameModel } from "./model.js";
import { GameView } from "./view.js";
import { initializeI18n } from "./i18n.js";
import { SudokuController } from "./games/sudoku/controller.js";
import { ChessController } from "./games/chess/controller.js";
import { BattleshipController } from "./games/battleship/controller.js";
import { KillerSudokuController } from "./games/killersudoku/controller.js";
import { A1B2Model } from "./games/1a2b/model.js";
import { A1B2View } from "./games/1a2b/view.js";
import { A1B2Controller } from "./games/1a2b/controller.js";
import { TicTacToeModel } from "./games/tictactoe/model.js";
import { TicTacToeView } from "./games/tictactoe/view.js";
import { TicTacToeController } from "./games/tictactoe/controller.js";
import { DotsAndBoxesModel } from "./games/dotsandboxes/model.js";
import { DotsAndBoxesView } from "./games/dotsandboxes/view.js";
import { DotsAndBoxesController } from "./games/dotsandboxes/controller.js";
import { YahtzeeModel } from "./games/yahtzee/model.js";
import { YahtzeeView } from "./games/yahtzee/view.js";
import { YahtzeeController } from "./games/yahtzee/controller.js";

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async () => {
      const i18n = await initializeI18n();
      (() => {
        const model = new GameModel();
        const state = model.state;
        const view = new GameView(document, i18n.t);
        const elements = view.elements;
        const a1b2Model = new A1B2Model();
        const ticTacToeModel = new TicTacToeModel(model);
        const dotsAndBoxesModel = new DotsAndBoxesModel(model);
        const yahtzeeModel = new YahtzeeModel(model);
        const a1b2View = new A1B2View(view);
        const ticTacToeView = new TicTacToeView(view);
        const dotsAndBoxesView = new DotsAndBoxesView(view);
        const yahtzeeView = new YahtzeeView(view);
        const brokerUrl = "wss://broker.hivemq.com:8884/mqtt";
        let client = null;
        let localTicTacToe = false;
        let localDotsBoxes = false;
        let pendingYahtzeeMove = false;
        let isRoomHost = false;
        let standaloneController = null;
        let standaloneGameType = "";
        let standalonePlayerId = "";
        let standaloneSubscriptions = new Set();
        let pendingStandaloneMessages = [];
        let a1b2Controller = null;
        let ticTacToeController = null;
        let dotsAndBoxesController = null;
        let yahtzeeController = null;

        const roomTopic = () => model.roomTopic();
        const playerTopic = (id) => model.playerTopic(id);
        const secretKey = () => model.secretKey();
        const setConnection = (connectionState, text) => {
          view.setConnection(connectionState, i18n.t(text));
        };
        const setBanner = (message, kind = "neutral") => {
          view.setBanner(i18n.t(message), kind);
        };
        function setMode(nextMode) {
          state.mode = nextMode;
          view.setMode(state.mode);
        }

        function disposeStandalone(clearPending = true) {
          standaloneController?.dispose?.();
          standaloneController = null;
          standaloneGameType = "";
          standalonePlayerId = "";
          standaloneSubscriptions.clear();
          if (clearPending) pendingStandaloneMessages = [];
          for (const root of [elements.sudokuGame, elements.killerSudokuGame, elements.chessGame, elements.battleshipGame]) {
            root.replaceChildren();
            root.hidden = true;
          }
        }

        function startStandaloneGame(gameType, mode = state.mode) {
          state.playerName = model.validName(elements.name.value) || "Player";
          elements.name.value = state.playerName;
          localStorage.setItem("fourfold.name", state.playerName);
          disposeStandalone(false);
          const roots = {
            sudoku: elements.sudokuGame,
            killersudoku: elements.killerSudokuGame,
            chess: elements.chessGame,
            battleship: elements.battleshipGame
          };
          const root = roots[gameType];
          if (!root) throw new RangeError(`Unknown standalone game: ${gameType}`);
          standaloneGameType = gameType;
          root.hidden = false;
          state.gameType = gameType;
          state.mode = mode;
          state.soloMode = false;
          state.room = mode === "1P" ? "" : state.room;
          elements.setup.hidden = true;
          elements.lobby.hidden = true;
          elements.page.classList.add("is-playing");
          elements.game.hidden = false;
          elements.standaloneError.hidden = true;
          elements.game.dataset.mode = mode;
          elements.game.dataset.game = gameType;
          elements.roomHeading.hidden = true;
          elements.gameGrid.hidden = true;
          elements.ticTacToeGame.hidden = true;
          elements.dotsAndBoxesGame.hidden = true;
          elements.yahtzeeGame.hidden = true;
          elements.sudokuGame.hidden = gameType !== "sudoku";
          elements.killerSudokuGame.hidden = gameType !== "killersudoku";
          elements.chessGame.hidden = gameType !== "chess";
          elements.battleshipGame.hidden = gameType !== "battleship";
          const players = getPlayers();
          const localSeat = mode === "1P" || players[0]?.id === state.playerId ? "p1" : "p2";
          standalonePlayerId = localSeat;
          const transport = mode === "2P" ? {
            subscribe(listener) {
              standaloneSubscriptions.add(listener);
              return () => standaloneSubscriptions.delete(listener);
            },
            publish(message) {
              const payload = gameType === "battleship"
                ? message
                : gameType === "chess"
                  ? { game: gameType, playerId: localSeat, snapshot: message }
                  : { game: gameType, ...message };
              return publish(`${roomTopic()}/game`, payload, gameType !== "battleship");
            }
          } : null;
          const options = {
            root,
            mode,
            playerId: localSeat,
            playerName: state.playerName,
            transport,
            onExit: returnHome,
            t: i18n.t,
            onError: (error) => {
              elements.standaloneError.textContent = i18n.t("Could not update the game: {error}", {
                error: error.message
              });
              elements.standaloneError.hidden = false;
            }
          };
          standaloneController = gameType === "sudoku"
            ? new SudokuController(options)
            : gameType === "killersudoku"
              ? new KillerSudokuController(options)
            : gameType === "chess"
              ? new ChessController(options)
              : new BattleshipController(options);
          standaloneController.start();
          for (const data of pendingStandaloneMessages.splice(0)) {
            if (data.playerId === standalonePlayerId) continue;
            const message = gameType === "chess" ? data.snapshot : data;
            for (const listener of standaloneSubscriptions) listener(message);
          }
          i18n.apply();
        }

        function selectStandaloneGame(gameType) {
          state.gameType = gameType;
          localTicTacToe = false;
          localDotsBoxes = false;
          if (state.mode === "1P") {
            startStandaloneGame(gameType, "1P");
            return;
          }
          view.setGameType(gameType);
          elements.roomActions.hidden = false;
          elements.roomWaiting.hidden = true;
          elements.lobby.hidden = false;
          elements.roomError.textContent = "";
        }

        async function submitDotsMove(edge) {
          await dotsAndBoxesController.move(edge);
        }
        function startSoloGame() {
          state.playerName = model.validName(elements.name.value) || "Player";
          elements.name.value = state.playerName;
          localStorage.setItem("fourfold.name", state.playerName);
          state.soloMode = true;
          elements.page.classList.add("is-playing");
          state.soloSecret = a1b2Model.createSecret();
          state.room = "";
          state.playerId = "solo";
          state.players = { solo: { id: "solo", name: state.playerName, online: true, ready: true } };
          state.guesses = {};
          state.results = {};
          state.handledResults = new Set();
          state.gameState = { phase: "playing", turn: 1, activePlayerId: state.playerId };
          elements.setup.hidden = true;
          elements.lobby.hidden = true;
          elements.game.hidden = false;
          elements.standaloneError.hidden = true;
          elements.game.dataset.mode = "1P";
          elements.game.dataset.game = "1a2b";
          elements.game.setAttribute("aria-label", "1A2B game");
          elements.gameTitle.textContent = "1A2B";
          elements.modeBadge.textContent = "1P Â· Solo";
          elements.playersCard.hidden = true;
          elements.secretCard.hidden = true;
          elements.soloSecret.hidden = false;
          setBanner("Find the secret. Every clue counts.");
          render();
        }
        function startDotsAndBoxesSoloGame() {
          state.playerName = model.validName(elements.name.value) || "Player";
          elements.name.value = state.playerName;
          localStorage.setItem("fourfold.name", state.playerName);
          state.gameType = "dotsandboxes";
          state.room = "";
          state.playerId = "local";
          state.players = {
            local: { id: "local", name: state.playerName, online: true },
            computer: { id: "computer", name: "Computer", online: true }
          };
          state.gameState = dotsAndBoxesModel.createGame([
            { id: "local", name: state.playerName },
            { id: "computer", name: "Computer" }
          ]);
          state.joined = false;
          state.soloMode = false;
          localDotsBoxes = true;
          elements.setup.hidden = true;
          elements.lobby.hidden = true;
          elements.page.classList.add("is-playing");
          elements.game.hidden = false;
          elements.game.dataset.mode = "1P";
          elements.game.dataset.game = "dotsandboxes";
          elements.game.setAttribute("aria-label", "Dots and Boxes against the computer");
          elements.roomHeading.hidden = true;
          elements.gameGrid.hidden = true;
          elements.ticTacToeGame.hidden = true;
          elements.dotsAndBoxesGame.hidden = false;
          render();
        }
        function startYahtzeeGame() {
          state.playerName = model.validName(elements.name.value) || "Player";
          elements.name.value = state.playerName;
          localStorage.setItem("fourfold.name", state.playerName);
          state.gameType = "yahtzee";
          view.setGameType(state.gameType);
          if (state.mode === "2P") {
            elements.roomActions.hidden = false;
            elements.roomWaiting.hidden = true;
            elements.lobby.hidden = false;
            elements.roomError.textContent = "";
            return;
          }
          state.room = "";
          state.playerId = "solo";
          state.players = { solo: { id: "solo", name: state.playerName, online: true } };
          state.gameState = yahtzeeModel.createGame();
          state.joined = false;
          state.soloMode = false;
          localDotsBoxes = false;
          pendingYahtzeeMove = false;
          isRoomHost = false;
          localTicTacToe = false;
          elements.setup.hidden = true;
          elements.lobby.hidden = true;
          elements.page.classList.add("is-playing");
          elements.game.hidden = false;
          elements.game.dataset.mode = "1P";
          elements.game.dataset.game = "yahtzee";
          elements.game.setAttribute("aria-label", "å¿«è‰‡éª°å­ solo game");
          elements.roomHeading.hidden = true;
          elements.gameGrid.hidden = true;
          elements.ticTacToeGame.hidden = true;
          elements.dotsAndBoxesGame.hidden = true;
          elements.yahtzeeGame.hidden = false;
          render();
        }
        function startTicTacToeSoloGame() {
          state.playerName = model.validName(elements.name.value) || "Player";
          elements.name.value = state.playerName;
          localStorage.setItem("fourfold.name", state.playerName);
          state.gameType = "tictactoe";
          state.room = "";
          state.playerId = "local";
          state.players = {
            local: { id: "local", name: state.playerName, online: true },
            computer: { id: "computer", name: "Computer", online: true }
          };
          state.gameState = ticTacToeModel.createGame([
            { id: "local", name: state.playerName },
            { id: "computer", name: "Computer" }
          ]);
          state.joined = false;
          state.soloMode = false;
          localTicTacToe = true;
          elements.setup.hidden = true;
          elements.lobby.hidden = true;
          elements.page.classList.add("is-playing");
          elements.game.hidden = false;
          elements.game.dataset.mode = "1P";
          elements.game.dataset.game = "tictactoe";
          elements.game.setAttribute("aria-label", "Tic Tac Toe against the computer");
          elements.roomHeading.hidden = true;
          elements.gameGrid.hidden = true;
          elements.ticTacToeGame.hidden = false;
          render();
        }
        const publish = (topic, value, retain = true) => new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
          if (!client || !client.connected) {
            reject(new Error("The MQTT connection is not available."));
            return;
          }
          client.publish(topic, JSON.stringify(value), { qos: 1, retain }, (error) => {
            if (error) reject(error);
            else resolve();
          });
        });
        a1b2Controller = new A1B2Controller({
          model: a1b2Model,
          getState: () => state,
          render: () => render(),
          publish: (path, message) => publish(`${roomTopic()}/${path}`, message),
          onBanner: (message, kind) => setBanner(message, kind)
        });
        ticTacToeController = new TicTacToeController({
          model: ticTacToeModel,
          getState: () => state,
          isLocal: () => localTicTacToe,
          publish: (message) => publish(`${roomTopic()}/game`, message),
          render: () => render(),
          onError: (error) => { elements.ticTacToeStatus.textContent = `Could not send your move: ${error.message}`; }
        });
        dotsAndBoxesController = new DotsAndBoxesController({
          model: dotsAndBoxesModel,
          getState: () => state,
          isLocal: () => localDotsBoxes,
          publish: (message) => publish(`${roomTopic()}/game`, message),
          render: () => render(),
          onError: (error) => { elements.dotsStatus.textContent = `Could not send your move: ${error.message}`; }
        });
        yahtzeeController = new YahtzeeController({
          model: yahtzeeModel,
          getState: () => state,
          updateGame: (nextGame) => updateYahtzeeGame(nextGame),
          render: () => render()
        });

        function setRoomInput(value) {
          elements.room.value = value;
        }

        function enterRoom(code) {
          if (state.joining || state.joined) return;
          if (!window.mqtt) {
            setConnection("error", "Game library unavailable");
            setBanner("The MQTT library did not load. Check your connection and refresh the page.", "error");
            return;
          }
          state.room = code.toUpperCase();
          state.playerName = model.validName(elements.name.value) || "Player";
          const playerKey = state.gameType === "1a2b"
            ? `fourfold.player.${state.room}`
            : `fourfold.player.${state.gameType}.${state.room}`;
          state.playerId = localStorage.getItem(playerKey) || crypto.randomUUID().replaceAll("-", "").slice(0, 12);
          localStorage.setItem(playerKey, state.playerId);
          state.mySecret = sessionStorage.getItem(secretKey()) || "";
          state.joining = true;
          elements.create.disabled = true;
          elements.join.disabled = true;
          elements.room.value = state.room;
          setConnection("connecting", "Connecting to roomâ€¦");
          setBanner("Connecting to the game roomâ€¦");

          client = window.mqtt.connect(brokerUrl, {
            clientId: `fourfold-${crypto.randomUUID().replaceAll("-", "").slice(0, 18)}`,
            clean: true,
            connectTimeout: 12000,
            reconnectPeriod: 2000,
            will: {
              topic: playerTopic(state.playerId),
              payload: JSON.stringify({ id: state.playerId, online: false }),
              qos: 1,
              retain: true
            }
          });
          client.on("connect", () => {
            setConnection("connected", "Connected");
            client.subscribe(`${roomTopic()}/#`, { qos: 1 }, (error) => {
              if (error) {
                elements.roomError.textContent = "Could not join this room.";
                setBanner("Could not subscribe to the room. Please leave and try again.", "error");
                return;
              }
              if (state.joined) {
                const current = state.players[state.playerId];
                if (current) {
                  publish(playerTopic(state.playerId), { ...current, online: true })
                    .catch((publishError) => setBanner(`Could not restore your room status: ${publishError.message}`, "error"));
                }
              } else {
                window.setTimeout(completeJoin, 700);
              }
            });
          });
          client.on("reconnect", () => setConnection("connecting", "Reconnectingâ€¦"));
          client.on("offline", () => setConnection("connecting", "Reconnectingâ€¦"));
          client.on("close", () => {
            if (state.joined) setConnection("connecting", "Connection lost");
          });
          client.on("error", (error) => {
            setConnection("error", "Connection error");
            elements.roomError.textContent = "Connection failed. Try again.";
            const message = `Could not connect to the public MQTT broker: ${error.message || "unknown error"}`;
            if (state.gameType === "tictactoe" && !elements.game.hidden) {
              elements.ticTacToeStatus.textContent = message;
            } else if (state.gameType === "dotsandboxes" && !elements.game.hidden) {
              elements.dotsStatus.textContent = message;
            } else {
              setBanner(message, "error");
            }
          });
          client.on("message", onMessage);
        }

        async function completeJoin() {
          if (!client || !client.connected || state.joined) return;
          const otherPlayers = Object.values(state.players).filter((player) => player.id !== state.playerId);
          if (!state.players[state.playerId] && otherPlayers.length >= 2) {
            elements.roomError.textContent = "Room is full.";
            state.joining = false;
            elements.create.disabled = false;
            elements.join.disabled = false;
            client.end(true);
            client = null;
            return;
          }
          const previous = state.players[state.playerId] || {};
          const presence = {
            id: state.playerId,
            name: state.playerName,
            online: true,
            ready: state.gameType !== "1a2b" || Boolean(previous.ready),
            joinedAt: previous.joinedAt || Date.now()
          };
          try {
            await publish(playerTopic(state.playerId), presence);
            state.players[state.playerId] = presence;
            state.joined = true;
            state.joining = false;
            elements.setup.hidden = true;
            elements.roomActions.hidden = true;
            elements.roomWaiting.hidden = false;
            elements.roomWaitingCode.textContent = state.room;
            elements.lobby.hidden = false;
            elements.game.hidden = true;
            elements.standaloneError.hidden = true;
            state.soloMode = false;
            elements.game.dataset.mode = "2P";
            elements.game.dataset.game = state.gameType;
            const isBoardGame = state.gameType !== "1a2b";
            elements.game.setAttribute("aria-label", state.gameType === "tictactoe"
              ? "Tic Tac Toe game"
              : state.gameType === "dotsandboxes" ? "Dots and Boxes game"
                : state.gameType === "yahtzee" ? "Yahtzee game" : "1A2B game");
            elements.gameTitle.textContent = state.gameType === "tictactoe"
              ? "Tic Tac Toe"
              : state.gameType === "dotsandboxes" ? "é»žæ ¼æ£‹"
                : state.gameType === "yahtzee" ? "å¿«è‰‡éª°å­" : "1A2B";
            elements.modeBadge.textContent = "2P Â· Friends";
            elements.playersCard.hidden = isBoardGame;
            elements.secretCard.hidden = state.gameType !== "1a2b";
            elements.soloSecret.hidden = true;
            elements.code.textContent = state.room;
            elements.ticTacToeRoomCode.textContent = state.room;
            elements.dotsRoomCode.textContent = state.room;
            setBanner(isBoardGame
              ? "Waiting for a friend to join."
              : "Set your secret code. Once both players are ready, take turns guessing each otherâ€™s code.");
            render();
            updateRoomWaiting();
            maybeStartGame();
          } catch (error) {
            elements.roomError.textContent = "Could not join the room. Try again.";
            state.joining = false;
            elements.create.disabled = false;
            elements.join.disabled = false;
          }
        }

        function onMessage(topic, payload) {
          let data;
          try {
            data = JSON.parse(payload.toString());
          } catch {
            return;
          }
          const base = roomTopic();
          const relative = topic.slice(base.length + 1);
          const parts = relative.split("/");
          if (parts[0] === "players" && parts.length === 3 && parts[2] === "presence" && data.id) {
            const previous = state.players[data.id] || {};
            state.players[data.id] = { ...previous, ...data };
            if (!data.online && data.id !== state.playerId) state.players[data.id].ready = Boolean(previous.ready);
            renderPlayers();
            updateRoomWaiting();
            maybeStartGame();
            if (state.gameType !== "1a2b") render();
            return;
          }
          if (parts[0] === "game" && data?.game === state.gameType &&
            ["sudoku", "killersudoku", "chess", "battleship"].includes(state.gameType)) {
            if (data.playerId === standalonePlayerId) return;
            if (standaloneSubscriptions.size) {
              const message = state.gameType === "chess" ? data.snapshot : data;
              for (const listener of standaloneSubscriptions) listener(message);
            } else {
              pendingStandaloneMessages = [data];
            }
            return;
          }
          if (parts[0] === "game" && data && typeof data.turn === "number") {
            const currentEpoch = Number(state.gameState?.restartAt || 0);
            const incomingEpoch = Number(data.restartAt || 0);
            const isRestart = incomingEpoch > currentEpoch;
            if (isRestart) {
              state.guesses = {};
              state.results = {};
              state.handledResults = new Set();
            }
            if (!state.gameState || isRestart ||
              (incomingEpoch === currentEpoch && (data.turn > state.gameState.turn ||
                (data.turn === state.gameState.turn && state.gameState.phase !== "won" && data.phase === "won")))) {
              state.gameState = data;
              render();
            }
            return;
          }
          if (state.gameType === "tictactoe" && parts[0] === "game" &&
            data && Array.isArray(data.board) && typeof data.moveCount === "number") {
            const currentEpoch = Number(state.gameState?.restartAt || 0);
            const incomingEpoch = Number(data.restartAt || 0);
            if (!state.gameState || incomingEpoch > currentEpoch ||
              (incomingEpoch === currentEpoch && data.moveCount > state.gameState.moveCount)) {
              state.gameState = data;
              render();
            }
            return;
          }
          if (state.gameType === "dotsandboxes" && parts[0] === "game" &&
            dotsAndBoxesModel.isGame(data) &&
            typeof data.moveCount === "number") {
            const currentEpoch = Number(state.gameState?.restartAt || 0);
            const incomingEpoch = Number(data.restartAt || 0);
            if (!state.gameState || incomingEpoch > currentEpoch ||
              (incomingEpoch === currentEpoch && data.moveCount > state.gameState.moveCount)) {
              state.gameState = data;
              render();
            }
            return;
          }
          if (state.gameType === "yahtzee" && parts[0] === "game" &&
            data?.mode === "2P" && Array.isArray(data.players) && data.scorecards &&
            typeof data.moveCount === "number") {
            const current = state.gameState;
            const currentEpoch = Number(current?.restartAt || 0);
            const incomingEpoch = Number(data.restartAt || 0);
            if (!current || incomingEpoch > currentEpoch ||
              (incomingEpoch === currentEpoch && data.moveCount > current.moveCount)) {
              state.gameState = data;
              render();
            }
            return;
          }
          if (parts[0] === "moves" && parts.length === 3) {
            const turn = Number(parts[1]);
            if (!Number.isInteger(turn) || turn < 1) return;
            if (parts[2] === "guess" && typeof data.guess === "string" && data.by) {
              state.guesses[turn] = data;
              render();
              if (data.to === state.playerId && state.gameState?.turn === turn && state.mySecret) {
                answerGuess(turn, data);
              } else if (data.to === state.playerId && !state.mySecret) {
                setBanner("Your secret was lost when this page reloaded. Re-enter it below to continue.", "error");
              }
            } else if (parts[2] === "result" && typeof data.a === "number" && typeof data.b === "number") {
              state.results[turn] = data;
              render();
            }
          }
        }

        function getPlayers() {
          return model.getPlayers();
        }

        function updateRoomWaiting() {
          if (!state.joined) return;
          const onlinePlayers = getPlayers();
          if (onlinePlayers.length < 2) {
            elements.roomWaitingStatus.textContent = "Waiting for a player to join your room.";
            return;
          }
          elements.lobby.hidden = true;
          elements.game.hidden = false;
          elements.page.classList.add("is-playing");
          render();
        }

        function maybeStartGame() {
          if (!state.joined || state.gameState || getPlayers().length !== 2) return;
          const activePlayers = getPlayers();
          if (["sudoku", "killersudoku", "chess", "battleship"].includes(state.gameType)) {
            if (!standaloneController) startStandaloneGame(state.gameType, "2P");
            return;
          }
          if (state.gameType === "yahtzee") {
            if (!isRoomHost) return;
            state.gameState = yahtzeeModel.createMatch(activePlayers);
            publish(`${roomTopic()}/game`, state.gameState).catch((error) =>
              setBanner(`Could not start the game: ${error.message}`, "error")
            );
            render();
            return;
          }
          if (state.gameType === "tictactoe" || state.gameType === "dotsandboxes") {
            state.gameState = state.gameType === "dotsandboxes"
              ? dotsAndBoxesModel.createGame(activePlayers)
              : ticTacToeModel.createGame(activePlayers);
            publish(`${roomTopic()}/game`, state.gameState)
              .catch((error) => {
                const status = state.gameType === "dotsandboxes" ? elements.dotsStatus : elements.ticTacToeStatus;
                status.textContent = `Could not start the game: ${error.message}`;
              });
            render();
            return;
          }
          if (!activePlayers.every((player) => player.ready)) return;
          state.gameState = { phase: "playing", turn: 1, activePlayerId: activePlayers[0].id, startedAt: Date.now() };
          setBanner("Both players are ready. The first turn starts now.");
          publish(`${roomTopic()}/game`, state.gameState).catch((error) => setBanner(`Could not start the game: ${error.message}`, "error"));
          render();
        }

        function renderPlayers() {
          view.renderPlayers(state, getPlayers);
          i18n.apply();
        }

        function render() {
          if (["sudoku", "killersudoku", "chess", "battleship"].includes(state.gameType)) {
            elements.roomHeading.hidden = true;
            elements.gameGrid.hidden = true;
            elements.ticTacToeGame.hidden = true;
            elements.dotsAndBoxesGame.hidden = true;
            elements.yahtzeeGame.hidden = true;
            if (standaloneGameType === state.gameType) i18n.apply();
            return;
          }
          if (state.gameType === "tictactoe") {
            elements.roomHeading.hidden = true;
            elements.gameGrid.hidden = true;
            elements.ticTacToeGame.hidden = false;
            elements.dotsAndBoxesGame.hidden = true;
            elements.yahtzeeGame.hidden = true;
            elements.sudokuGame.hidden = true;
            elements.killerSudokuGame.hidden = true;
            elements.chessGame.hidden = true;
            elements.battleshipGame.hidden = true;
            ticTacToeView.render(state, getPlayers, ticTacToeController.pending);
            i18n.apply();
            return;
          }
          if (state.gameType === "dotsandboxes") {
            elements.roomHeading.hidden = true;
            elements.gameGrid.hidden = true;
            elements.ticTacToeGame.hidden = true;
            elements.dotsAndBoxesGame.hidden = false;
            elements.yahtzeeGame.hidden = true;
            elements.sudokuGame.hidden = true;
            elements.killerSudokuGame.hidden = true;
            elements.chessGame.hidden = true;
            elements.battleshipGame.hidden = true;
            dotsAndBoxesView.render(state, getPlayers, dotsAndBoxesController.pending, localDotsBoxes);
            i18n.apply();
            return;
          }
          if (state.gameType === "yahtzee") {
            elements.roomHeading.hidden = true;
            elements.gameGrid.hidden = true;
            elements.ticTacToeGame.hidden = true;
            elements.dotsAndBoxesGame.hidden = true;
            elements.yahtzeeGame.hidden = false;
            elements.sudokuGame.hidden = true;
            elements.killerSudokuGame.hidden = true;
            elements.chessGame.hidden = true;
            elements.battleshipGame.hidden = true;
            yahtzeeView.render(state, yahtzeeModel, pendingYahtzeeMove);
            i18n.apply();
            return;
          }
          elements.roomHeading.hidden = false;
          elements.gameGrid.hidden = false;
          elements.ticTacToeGame.hidden = true;
          elements.dotsAndBoxesGame.hidden = true;
          elements.yahtzeeGame.hidden = true;
          elements.sudokuGame.hidden = true;
          elements.killerSudokuGame.hidden = true;
          elements.chessGame.hidden = true;
          elements.battleshipGame.hidden = true;
          a1b2View.render(state, getPlayers, () => a1b2Controller.processCurrentResult());
          i18n.apply();
        }

        async function updateYahtzeeGame(nextGame) {
          if (!nextGame || pendingYahtzeeMove) return;
          const previousGame = state.gameState;
          state.gameState = nextGame;
          if (nextGame.mode !== "2P" || !state.room) {
            render();
            return;
          }
          pendingYahtzeeMove = true;
          render();
          try {
            await publish(`${roomTopic()}/game`, nextGame);
          } catch (error) {
            if (state.gameState === nextGame) state.gameState = previousGame;
            elements.yahtzeeHint.textContent = i18n.t("Could not update the game: {error}", {
              error: error.message
            });
          } finally {
            pendingYahtzeeMove = false;
            render();
          }
        }

        function rollYahtzeeDice() {
          yahtzeeController.roll();
        }

        function toggleYahtzeeDie(dieIndex) {
          yahtzeeController.toggleDie(dieIndex);
        }

        function scoreYahtzeeCategory(categoryId) {
          yahtzeeController.score(categoryId);
        }

        async function submitTicTacToeMove(cell) {
          await ticTacToeController.move(cell);
        }

        async function submitGuess(value) {
          if (!a1b2Model.isValidCode(value)) {
            setBanner("Use 4 different digits.", "error");
            elements.guess.focus();
            return;
          }
          if (state.soloMode) {
            submitSoloGuess(value);
            return;
          }
          if (!state.gameState || state.gameState.phase !== "playing" ||
            state.gameState.activePlayerId !== state.playerId || state.guesses[state.gameState.turn]) return;
          const turn = state.gameState.turn;
          const guess = {
            turn,
            guess: value,
            by: state.playerId,
            to: getPlayers().find((player) => player.id !== state.playerId)?.id,
            at: Date.now()
          };
          state.guesses[turn] = guess;
          elements.guess.value = "";
          render();
          try {
            await publish(`${roomTopic()}/moves/${turn}/guess`, guess);
          } catch (error) {
            delete state.guesses[turn];
            setBanner(`Could not send your guess: ${error.message}`, "error");
            render();
          }
        }

        function submitSoloGuess(value) {
          a1b2Controller.submitSoloGuess(value);
          elements.guess.value = "";
        }

        async function answerGuess(turn, guess) {
          if (!client?.connected) return;
          await a1b2Controller.answerGuess(turn, guess);
        }

        elements.modeOne.addEventListener("click", () => setMode("1P"));
        elements.modeTwo.addEventListener("click", () => setMode("2P"));
        elements.gameTile.addEventListener("click", () => {
          state.gameType = "1a2b";
          view.setGameType(state.gameType);
          if (state.mode === "1P") startSoloGame();
          else {
            elements.roomActions.hidden = false;
            elements.roomWaiting.hidden = true;
            elements.lobby.hidden = false;
            elements.roomError.textContent = "";
          }
        });
        elements.ticTacToeTile.addEventListener("click", () => {
          state.gameType = "tictactoe";
          localTicTacToe = false;
          if (state.mode === "1P") {
            startTicTacToeSoloGame();
            return;
          }
          view.setGameType(state.gameType);
          elements.roomActions.hidden = false;
          elements.roomWaiting.hidden = true;
          elements.lobby.hidden = false;
          elements.roomError.textContent = "";
        });
        elements.dotsAndBoxesTile.addEventListener("click", () => {
          state.gameType = "dotsandboxes";
          localDotsBoxes = false;
          if (state.mode === "1P") {
            startDotsAndBoxesSoloGame();
            return;
          }
          view.setGameType(state.gameType);
          elements.roomActions.hidden = false;
          elements.roomWaiting.hidden = true;
          elements.lobby.hidden = false;
          elements.roomError.textContent = "";
        });
        elements.sudokuTile.addEventListener("click", () => selectStandaloneGame("sudoku"));
        elements.killerSudokuTile.addEventListener("click", () => selectStandaloneGame("killersudoku"));
        elements.chessTile.addEventListener("click", () => selectStandaloneGame("chess"));
        elements.battleshipTile.addEventListener("click", () => selectStandaloneGame("battleship"));
        elements.yahtzeeTile.addEventListener("click", startYahtzeeGame);
        elements.create.addEventListener("click", () => {
          isRoomHost = true;
          if (!window.mqtt) {
            elements.roomError.textContent = "Could not load online play.";
            return;
          }
          const code = model.createRoomCode();
          elements.room.value = code;
          enterRoom(code);
        });
        elements.join.addEventListener("click", () => {
          isRoomHost = false;
          const code = elements.room.value.trim().toUpperCase();
          if (!model.isValidRoomCode(code)) {
            elements.roomError.textContent = "Enter a 6-digit room code.";
            return;
          }
          elements.roomError.textContent = "";
          enterRoom(code);
        });
        elements.room.addEventListener("input", () => {
          elements.room.value = elements.room.value.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 6);
        });
        elements.secretForm.addEventListener("submit", async (event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          const value = elements.secret.value;
          if (!a1b2Model.isValidCode(value)) {
            setBanner("Your secret must be four digits with no repeats.", "error");
            elements.secret.focus();
            return;
          }
          if (!state.joined || !client?.connected) {
            setBanner("Reconnect to the room before setting your secret.", "error");
            return;
          }
          state.mySecret = value;
          sessionStorage.setItem(secretKey(), value);
          const current = state.players[state.playerId] || { id: state.playerId, name: state.playerName, joinedAt: Date.now() };
          const presence = { ...current, id: state.playerId, name: state.playerName, ready: true, online: true };
          try {
            await publish(playerTopic(state.playerId), presence);
            state.players[state.playerId] = presence;
            setBanner("Your secret is saved on this device only. Waiting for your opponent to ready up.");
            render();
            maybeStartGame();
          } catch (error) {
            setBanner(`Could not mark you ready: ${error.message}`, "error");
          }
        });
        function updateGuessSlots() {
          a1b2View.updateGuessSlots(state);
        }
        const unmountA1B2Handlers = a1b2View.mountHandlers({
          onSubmit: submitGuess,
          restart: elements.restart,
          onRestart: () => a1b2Controller.restart(getPlayers(), state.soloMode),
          onDigit: (digit) => {
            if (elements.guess.value.length < 4) elements.guess.value += digit;
            updateGuessSlots();
          },
          onClear: () => { elements.guess.value = ""; updateGuessSlots(); },
          onBackspace: () => { elements.guess.value = elements.guess.value.slice(0, -1); updateGuessSlots(); },
          onInput: () => {
            elements.guess.value = elements.guess.value.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 4);
            updateGuessSlots();
          }
        });
        const unmountTicTacToeHandlers = ticTacToeView.mountHandlers(
          elements.ticTacToeBoard,
          (cell) => submitTicTacToeMove(cell),
          elements.ticTacToeRestart,
          () => ticTacToeController.restart()
        );
        const unmountDotsHandlers = dotsAndBoxesView.mountHandlers(
          elements.dotsBoard,
          (edge) => submitDotsMove(edge),
          elements.dotsRestart,
          () => dotsAndBoxesController.restart()
        );
        const unmountYahtzeeHandlers = yahtzeeView.mountHandlers({
          dice: elements.yahtzeeDice,
          roll: elements.yahtzeeRoll,
          scoreList: elements.yahtzeeScoreList,
          restart: elements.yahtzeeRestart,
          onToggleDie: toggleYahtzeeDie,
          onRoll: rollYahtzeeDice,
          onScore: scoreYahtzeeCategory,
          onRestart: () => yahtzeeController.restart()
        });
        elements.roomWaitingCopy.addEventListener("click", async () => {
          try {
            await navigator.clipboard.writeText(state.room);
            elements.roomWaitingCopy.textContent = "Copied";
            window.setTimeout(() => { elements.roomWaitingCopy.textContent = "Copy room code"; }, 1500);
          } catch (error) {
            elements.roomWaitingStatus.textContent = `Could not copy the room code: ${error.message}. Share ${state.room} with your friend.`;
          }
        });
        function returnHome() {
          disposeStandalone();
          if (client) {
            const currentClient = client;
            if (state.joined && currentClient.connected) {
              publish(playerTopic(state.playerId), { ...state.players[state.playerId], online: false })
                .catch((error) => console.error("Could not clear player presence:", error))
                .finally(() => currentClient.end(true));
            } else {
              currentClient.end(true);
            }
          }
          client = null;
          localTicTacToe = false;
          localDotsBoxes = false;
          model.resetGame();
          elements.setup.hidden = false;
          elements.page.classList.remove("is-playing");
          elements.lobby.hidden = true;
          elements.roomActions.hidden = false;
          elements.roomWaiting.hidden = true;
          elements.roomWaitingCopy.textContent = "Copy room code";
          elements.roomWaitingCode.textContent = "------";
          elements.game.hidden = true;
          elements.standaloneError.hidden = true;
          elements.game.dataset.mode = "";
          elements.game.dataset.game = "";
          elements.create.disabled = false;
          elements.join.disabled = false;
          elements.secret.value = "";
          elements.guess.value = "";
          updateGuessSlots();
          elements.playersCard.hidden = false;
          elements.secretCard.hidden = false;
          elements.soloSecret.hidden = true;
          elements.roomError.textContent = "";
          elements.room.value = "";
          setConnection("offline", "Not connected");
          setBanner("");
          window.scrollTo(0, 0);
        }
        elements.backHome.addEventListener("click", returnHome);
        elements.leave.addEventListener("click", returnHome);
        elements.ticTacToeExit.addEventListener("click", returnHome);
        elements.dotsExit.addEventListener("click", returnHome);
        elements.yahtzeeExit.addEventListener("click", returnHome);

        if (!window.mqtt) {
          elements.create.disabled = true;
          elements.join.disabled = true;
          console.error("MQTT library unavailable; online multiplayer is disabled.");
        }
        elements.name.value = localStorage.getItem("fourfold.name") || "Alex";
        elements.greeting.textContent = elements.name.value;
        elements.editName.addEventListener("click", () => elements.name.focus());
        elements.name.addEventListener("change", () => {
          const name = model.validName(elements.name.value);
          elements.name.value = name;
          if (name) {
            localStorage.setItem("fourfold.name", name);
            elements.greeting.textContent = name;
          }
        });
        elements.name.addEventListener("input", () => {
          elements.greeting.textContent = model.validName(elements.name.value) || "friend";
        });
      })();
      });

FILE: \js\games\1a2b\controller.js
==================================================
export class A1B2Controller {
  constructor({ model, getState, render, publish, onBanner = () => {} } = {}) {
    if (!model || typeof getState !== "function" || typeof render !== "function") {
      throw new TypeError("A1B2Controller requires a model, state accessor, and renderer.");
    }
    this.model = model;
    this.getState = getState;
    this.render = render;
    this.publish = publish;
    this.onBanner = onBanner;
  }

  submitSoloGuess(value) {
    const state = this.getState();
    const turn = state.gameState.turn;
    const result = this.model.scoreGuess(state.soloSecret, value);
    state.guesses[turn] = { turn, guess: value, by: state.playerId, at: Date.now() };
    state.results[turn] = result;
    if (result.a === 4) state.gameState = { ...state.gameState, phase: "won" };
    else state.gameState = { ...state.gameState, turn: turn + 1 };
    this.render();
  }

  async answerGuess(turn, guess) {
    const state = this.getState();
    if (!state.mySecret || state.results[turn]) return;
    const { a, b } = this.model.scoreGuess(state.mySecret, guess.guess);
    try {
      await this.publish(`moves/${turn}/result`, {
        turn, a, b, by: state.playerId, for: guess.by, at: Date.now()
      });
    } catch (error) {
      this.onBanner(`Could not share the result: ${error.message}`, "error");
    }
  }

  processCurrentResult() {
    const state = this.getState();
    if (!state.gameState || state.gameState.phase !== "playing") return;
    const result = state.results[state.gameState.turn];
    if (!result || state.handledResults.has(state.gameState.turn)) return;
    state.handledResults.add(state.gameState.turn);
    if (result.a === 4) {
      state.gameState = { ...state.gameState, phase: "won", winnerId: result.for };
    } else {
      state.gameState = {
        ...state.gameState,
        turn: state.gameState.turn + 1,
        activePlayerId: result.by
      };
    }
    this.publish("game", state.gameState).catch((error) =>
      this.onBanner(`Could not update the game: ${error.message}`, "error"));
    this.render();
  }

  async restart(players, soloMode) {
    const state = this.getState();
    state.guesses = {};
    state.results = {};
    state.handledResults = new Set();
    if (soloMode) {
      state.soloSecret = this.model.createSecret();
      state.gameState = { phase: "playing", turn: 1, activePlayerId: state.playerId };
      this.render();
      return;
    }
    const previousGame = state.gameState;
    const nextGame = {
      phase: "playing",
      turn: 1,
      activePlayerId: players[0]?.id,
      startedAt: Date.now(),
      restartAt: Math.max(Date.now(), Number(previousGame?.restartAt || 0) + 1)
    };
    state.gameState = nextGame;
    this.render();
    try {
      await this.publish("game", nextGame);
    } catch (error) {
      if (state.gameState === nextGame) state.gameState = previousGame;
      this.onBanner(`Could not restart the game: ${error.message}`, "error");
      this.render();
    }
  }
}


==================================================
FILE: \js\games\1a2b\model.js
==================================================
export class A1B2Model {
  isValidCode(value) {
    return /^(?!.*(.).*\1)[0-9]{4}$/.test(value);
  }

  scoreGuess(secret, guess) {
    const exact = [...guess].filter((digit, index) => digit === secret[index]).length;
    const matching = [...guess].filter((digit) => secret.includes(digit)).length;
    return { a: exact, b: matching - exact };
  }

  createSecret(cryptoSource = globalThis.crypto) {
    const digits = "0123456789".split("");
    for (let index = digits.length - 1; index > 0; index -= 1) {
      const swap = cryptoSource.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(1))[0] % (index + 1);
      [digits[index], digits[swap]] = [digits[swap], digits[index]];
    }
    return digits.slice(0, 4).join("");
  }
}

export class A1B2View {
  constructor(gameView) {
    this.gameView = gameView;
  }

  render(state, getPlayers, onCurrentResult) {
    this.gameView.render(state, getPlayers, onCurrentResult);
  }

  mountHandlers({ onSubmit, onDigit, onClear, onBackspace, onInput, restart, onRestart } = {}) {
    const elements = this.gameView.elements;
    this.handlers = { onSubmit, onDigit, onClear, onBackspace, onInput, onRestart };
    this.handleFormSubmit = (event) => {
      event.preventDefault();
      this.handlers?.onSubmit?.(elements.guess.value);
    };
    this.handleKeypadClick = (event) => {
      const button = event.target.closest("button");
      if (!button || button.disabled) return;
      if (button.dataset.digit) this.handlers?.onDigit?.(button.dataset.digit);
      else if (button.dataset.action === "clear") this.handlers?.onClear?.();
      else if (button.dataset.action === "delete") this.handlers?.onBackspace?.();
    };
    this.handleInput = () => this.handlers?.onInput?.();
    this.handleRestart = () => this.handlers?.onRestart?.();
    elements.guessForm.addEventListener("submit", this.handleFormSubmit);
    elements.keypad.addEventListener("click", this.handleKeypadClick);
    elements.guess.addEventListener("input", this.handleInput);
    elements.keypadSubmit.addEventListener("click", this.handleFormSubmit);
    restart.addEventListener("click", this.handleRestart);
    return () => this.dispose();
  }

  updateGuessSlots(state) {
    this.gameView.updateGuessSlots(state);
  }

  dispose() {
    const elements = this.gameView.elements;
    elements.guessForm.removeEventListener("submit", this.handleFormSubmit);
    elements.keypad.removeEventListener("click", this.handleKeypadClick);
    elements.guess.removeEventListener("input", this.handleInput);
    elements.keypadSubmit.removeEventListener("click", this.handleFormSubmit);
    this.gameView.elements.restart.removeEventListener("click", this.handleRestart);
    this.handlers = null;
  }
}


==================================================
FILE: \js\games\battleship\controller.js
==================================================
import { BattleshipModel, BATTLESHIP_FLEET } from './model.js';
import { BattleshipView } from './view.js';

const PLAYER_IDS = ['p1', 'p2'];
const otherPlayer = (playerId) => playerId === 'p1' ? 'p2' : 'p1';

export class BattleshipController {
  constructor({
    root,
    mode = '1P',
    playerId = 'p1',
    transport = null,
    onExit = () => {},
    onError = () => {},
    t = (value) => value,
  } = {}) {
    if (!root) throw new TypeError('BattleshipController requires a root element');
    this.root = root;
    this.mode = mode === '2P' ? '2P' : '1P';
    this.playerId = this.mode === '1P' ? 'p1' : (PLAYER_IDS.includes(playerId) ? playerId : 'p1');
    this.transport = transport;
    this.onExit = onExit;
    this.onError = onError;
    this.t = t;
    this.model = new BattleshipModel({ mode: this.mode, playerId: this.playerId });
    this.readyPlayers = new Set();
    this.unsubscribe = null;
    this.started = false;
    this.disposed = false;
    this.view = new BattleshipView({
      root,
      t,
      onAction: (action) => this.handleAction(action),
    });
  }

  start() {
    if (this.disposed) return this;
    if (typeof this.unsubscribe === 'function') this.unsubscribe();
    this.unsubscribe = null;
    this.model.startNewGame({ mode: this.mode, playerId: this.playerId });
    this.readyPlayers.clear();
    this.started = false;
    if (this.mode === '2P' && typeof this.transport?.subscribe === 'function') {
      this.unsubscribe = this.transport.subscribe((message) => this.receive(message));
    }
    this.render();
    return this;
  }

  receive(message) {
    if (this.disposed || this.mode !== '2P' || !message || message.game !== 'battleship') return false;
    if (!PLAYER_IDS.includes(message.playerId) || message.playerId === this.playerId) return false;
    if (message.kind === 'ready') {
      this.readyPlayers.add(message.playerId);
      this._startIfReady();
      this.render();
      return true;
    }
    if (message.kind === 'restart') {
      this.model.startNewGame({ mode: this.mode, playerId: this.playerId });
      this.readyPlayers.clear();
      this.started = false;
      this.render();
      return true;
    }
    if (message.kind === 'start') {
      this.readyPlayers.add(message.playerId);
      this._startIfReady();
      return true;
    }
    if (message.kind === 'shot') {
      if (message.playerId !== this.model.state.currentPlayerId) return false;
      const result = this.model.fire(message.index, message.playerId);
      if (!result.accepted) return false;
      this._publish({
        kind: 'shot-result',
        playerId: this.playerId,
        by: message.playerId,
        index: message.index,
        result: result.result,
        sunkShipId: result.sunkShipId,
        winner: result.winner,
      });
      this.render();
      return true;
    }
    if (message.kind === 'shot-result') {
      if (message.playerId !== otherPlayer(this.playerId)
        || message.by !== this.playerId
        || !['hit', 'miss'].includes(message.result)) return false;
      if (!this.model.resolveShot(message.index, this.playerId, message.result, message.sunkShipId)) return false;
      if (message.winner && PLAYER_IDS.includes(message.winner)) {
        this.model.state.winner = message.winner;
        this.model.state.phase = 'finished';
      }
      this.render();
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  handleAction(action) {
    if (this.disposed || !action) return;
    switch (action.type) {
      case 'exit':
        this.onExit();
        break;
      case 'restart':
        this._restart();
        break;
      case 'select-ship':
        this.model.setSelectedShip(action.shipId);
        this.render();
        break;
      case 'drop-ship':
        this.model.setSelectedShip(action.shipId);
        this._handleCell({ board: 'own', index: action.index });
        break;
      case 'rotate':
        this.model.rotate();
        this.render();
        break;
      case 'auto-place':
        this.model.autoPlace(this.playerId);
        if (this.mode === '2P' && this._fleetReady()) this._markReady();
        this.render();
        break;
      case 'start':
        this._startSolo();
        break;
      case 'cell':
        this._handleCell(action);
        break;
      default:
        break;
    }
  }

  dispose() {
    if (this.disposed) return;
    this.disposed = true;
    if (typeof this.unsubscribe === 'function') this.unsubscribe();
    this.unsubscribe = null;
    this.view.dispose();
  }

  _handleCell(action) {
    if (this.model.state.phase === 'setup' && action.board === 'own') {
      const placed = this.model.placeShip(
        this.model.state.selectedShipId,
        action.index,
        this.model.state.orientation,
        this.playerId,
      );
      if (placed && this.mode === '2P' && this._fleetReady()) this._markReady();
      this.render();
      return;
    }
    if (this.model.state.phase !== 'playing' || action.board !== 'target') return;
    if (this.mode === '2P') {
      const result = this.model.fire(action.index, this.playerId);
      if (result.accepted) {
        this._publish({ kind: 'shot', playerId: this.playerId, index: action.index });
        this.render();
      }
      return;
    }

    const result = this.model.fire(action.index, this.playerId);
    if (!result.accepted) return;
    if (this.model.state.phase !== 'finished') {
      const computerIndex = this.model.chooseComputerShot();
      if (computerIndex !== null) this.model.fire(computerIndex, 'p2');
    }
    this.render();
  }

  _startSolo() {
    if (this.mode !== '1P' || this.model.state.phase !== 'setup') return;
    if (!this.model.state.players.p1.ready) return;
    this.model.autoPlace('p2');
    if (this.model.startGame()) this.render();
  }

  _markReady() {
    if (this.readyPlayers.has(this.playerId)) return;
    this.readyPlayers.add(this.playerId);
    this._publish({ kind: 'ready', playerId: this.playerId });
    this._startIfReady();
  }

  _startIfReady() {
    if (this.mode !== '2P' || this.started || !this._fleetReady()) return;
    if (!this.readyPlayers.has('p1') || !this.readyPlayers.has('p2')) return;
    this.started = this.model.startGame();
    if (this.started && this.playerId === 'p1') {
      this._publish({ kind: 'start', playerId: this.playerId });
    }
    this.render();
  }

  _fleetReady() {
    const ships = this.model.state.players[this.playerId].ships;
    return BATTLESHIP_FLEET.every((definition) =>
      ships.some((ship) => ship.id === definition.id && ship.coordinates.length === definition.length),
    );
  }

  _publish(message) {
    if (typeof this.transport?.publish !== 'function') return;
    try {
      const publication = this.transport.publish({ game: 'battleship', ...message });
      if (publication && typeof publication.catch === 'function') {
        publication.catch((error) => this.onError(error));
      }
    } catch (error) {
      this.onError(error);
    }
  }

  _restart() {
    this.model.startNewGame({ mode: this.mode, playerId: this.playerId });
    this.readyPlayers.clear();
    this.started = false;
    if (this.mode === '2P') this._publish({ kind: 'restart', playerId: this.playerId });
    this.render();
  }

  render() {
    this.view.render(this.model.getSnapshot({ privateFor: this.playerId }), this.playerId);
  }
}


==================================================
FILE: \js\games\battleship\model.js
==================================================
export const BATTLESHIP_FLEET = Object.freeze([
  Object.freeze({ id: 'carrier', length: 5 }),
  Object.freeze({ id: 'battleship', length: 4 }),
  Object.freeze({ id: 'cruiser', length: 3 }),
  Object.freeze({ id: 'submarine', length: 3 }),
  Object.freeze({ id: 'destroyer', length: 2 }),
]);

export const BATTLESHIP_BOARD_SIZE = 10;

const PLAYER_IDS = ['p1', 'p2'];
const CELL_COUNT = BATTLESHIP_BOARD_SIZE * BATTLESHIP_BOARD_SIZE;
const opponentOf = (playerId) => playerId === 'p1' ? 'p2' : 'p1';

function createShips() {
  return BATTLESHIP_FLEET.map(({ id, length }) => ({
    id,
    length,
    coordinates: [],
    orientation: 'horizontal',
    sunk: false,
  }));
}

function createPlayers() {
  return {
    p1: { ready: false, ships: createShips() },
    p2: { ready: false, ships: createShips() },
  };
}

function copyShot(shot) {
  return {
    index: shot.index,
    result: shot.result,
    shipId: shot.shipId || null,
    sunkShipId: shot.sunkShipId || null,
  };
}

export class BattleshipModel {
  constructor({ seed, mode = '1P', playerId = 'p1' } = {}) {
    this._initialSeed = seed;
    this._randomState = Number.isFinite(seed) ? seed >>> 0 : null;
    this.state = {};
    this.startNewGame({ mode, playerId });
  }

  startNewGame({ mode = this.state.mode || '1P', playerId = this.state.playerId || 'p1' } = {}) {
    if (this._initialSeed !== undefined) this._randomState = this._initialSeed >>> 0;
    this.state = {
      mode: mode === '2P' ? '2P' : '1P',
      playerId: PLAYER_IDS.includes(playerId) ? playerId : 'p1',
      phase: 'setup',
      currentPlayerId: 'p1',
      placementPlayerId: PLAYER_IDS.includes(playerId) ? playerId : 'p1',
      orientation: 'horizontal',
      selectedShipId: BATTLESHIP_FLEET[0].id,
      players: createPlayers(),
      shots: { p1: [], p2: [] },
      winner: null,
    };
    return this.state;
  }

  setSelectedShip(shipId) {
    if (BATTLESHIP_FLEET.some((ship) => ship.id === shipId)) {
      this.state.selectedShipId = shipId;
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  setPlacementPlayer(playerId) {
    if (!PLAYER_IDS.includes(playerId) || this.state.phase !== 'setup') return false;
    this.state.placementPlayerId = playerId;
    return true;
  }

  placeShip(shipId, index, orientation = this.state.orientation, playerId = this.state.placementPlayerId) {
    if (this.state.phase !== 'setup' || !PLAYER_IDS.includes(playerId)) return false;
    const ship = this.state.players[playerId].ships.find((item) => item.id === shipId);
    if (!ship || !Number.isInteger(index) || index < 0 || index >= CELL_COUNT) return false;
    if (orientation !== 'horizontal' && orientation !== 'vertical') return false;

    const row = Math.floor(index / BATTLESHIP_BOARD_SIZE);
    const column = index % BATTLESHIP_BOARD_SIZE;
    const coordinates = [];
    for (let offset = 0; offset < ship.length; offset += 1) {
      const nextRow = row + (orientation === 'vertical' ? offset : 0);
      const nextColumn = column + (orientation === 'horizontal' ? offset : 0);
      if (nextRow >= BATTLESHIP_BOARD_SIZE || nextColumn >= BATTLESHIP_BOARD_SIZE) return false;
      coordinates.push(nextRow * BATTLESHIP_BOARD_SIZE + nextColumn);
    }

    const occupied = new Set(
      this.state.players[playerId].ships
        .filter((item) => item.id !== shipId)
        .flatMap((item) => item.coordinates),
    );
    if (coordinates.some((cell) => occupied.has(cell))) return false;

    ship.coordinates = coordinates;
    ship.orientation = orientation;
    ship.sunk = false;
    this.state.players[playerId].ready = this._isFleetPlaced(playerId);
    return true;
  }

  rotate() {
    this.state.orientation = this.state.orientation === 'horizontal' ? 'vertical' : 'horizontal';
    return this.state.orientation;
  }

  autoPlace(playerId = this.state.placementPlayerId) {
    if (this.state.phase !== 'setup' || !PLAYER_IDS.includes(playerId)) return false;
    const ships = this.state.players[playerId].ships;
    for (const ship of ships) {
      ship.coordinates = [];
      ship.sunk = false;
    }

    for (const ship of ships) {
      const candidates = Array.from({ length: CELL_COUNT * 2 }, (_, index) => index);
      this._shuffle(candidates);
      let placed = false;
      for (const candidate of candidates) {
        const orientation = candidate >= CELL_COUNT ? 'vertical' : 'horizontal';
        const index = candidate % CELL_COUNT;
        if (this.placeShip(ship.id, index, orientation, playerId)) {
          placed = true;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (!placed) {
        for (const item of ships) item.coordinates = [];
        this.state.players[playerId].ready = false;
        return false;
      }
    }
    this.state.players[playerId].ready = true;
    return true;
  }

  startGame() {
    if (this.state.phase !== 'setup') return false;
    if (this.state.mode === '1P') {
      if (!this._isFleetPlaced('p1') || !this._isFleetPlaced('p2')) return false;
    } else if (!this._isFleetPlaced(this.state.playerId)) {
      return false;
    }
    this.state.phase = 'playing';
    this.state.currentPlayerId = 'p1';
    return true;
  }

  fire(index, playerId = this.state.playerId) {
    if (this.state.phase !== 'playing' || !PLAYER_IDS.includes(playerId)) {
      return { accepted: false, reason: 'not-playing' };
    }
    if (this.state.currentPlayerId !== playerId) return { accepted: false, reason: 'not-your-turn' };
    if (!Number.isInteger(index) || index < 0 || index >= CELL_COUNT) {
      return { accepted: false, reason: 'invalid-cell' };
    }
    if (this.state.shots[playerId].some((shot) => shot.index === index)) {
      return { accepted: false, reason: 'already-fired' };
    }

    const targetId = opponentOf(playerId);
    const targetShips = this.state.players[targetId].ships;
    const hasFleet = targetShips.some((ship) => ship.coordinates.length > 0);
    const shot = { index, result: hasFleet ? 'miss' : 'pending', shipId: null, sunkShipId: null };
    if (hasFleet) {
      const hitShip = targetShips.find((ship) => ship.coordinates.includes(index));
      if (hitShip) {
        shot.result = 'hit';
        shot.shipId = hitShip.id;
        const hitCells = new Set(
          this.state.shots[playerId]
            .filter((item) => item.result === 'hit' && item.shipId === hitShip.id)
            .map((item) => item.index),
        );
        hitCells.add(index);
        if (hitShip.coordinates.every((cell) => hitCells.has(cell))) {
          hitShip.sunk = true;
          shot.sunkShipId = hitShip.id;
        }
      }
    }

    this.state.shots[playerId].push(shot);
    this.state.currentPlayerId = targetId;
    if (shot.sunkShipId && targetShips.every((ship) => ship.sunk)) {
      this.state.winner = playerId;
      this.state.phase = 'finished';
    }
    return { accepted: true, ...copyShot(shot), by: playerId, targetId, winner: this.state.winner };
  }

  resolveShot(index, playerId, result, sunkShipId = null) {
    if (!PLAYER_IDS.includes(playerId) || !['hit', 'miss'].includes(result)) return false;
    const shot = [...this.state.shots[playerId]].reverse().find(
      (item) => item.index === index && item.result === 'pending',
    );
    if (!shot) return false;
    shot.result = result;
    shot.sunkShipId = sunkShipId || null;
    const targetId = opponentOf(playerId);
    if (sunkShipId) {
      const targetShip = this.state.players[targetId].ships.find((item) => item.id === sunkShipId);
      if (targetShip) targetShip.sunk = true;
    }
    this.state.currentPlayerId = targetId;
    if (sunkShipId && BATTLESHIP_FLEET.every(({ id }) =>
      this.state.players[targetId].ships.find((ship) => ship.id === id)?.sunk,
    )) {
      this.state.winner = playerId;
      this.state.phase = 'finished';
    }
    return true;
  }

  chooseComputerShot() {
    const playerId = 'p2';
    const tried = new Set(this.state.shots[playerId].map((shot) => shot.index));
    const available = (index) => index >= 0 && index < CELL_COUNT && !tried.has(index);
    const hitIndices = this.state.shots[playerId]
      .filter((shot) => shot.result === 'hit')
      .map((shot) => shot.index);
    const adjacent = [];
    for (const hit of hitIndices) {
      const row = Math.floor(hit / BATTLESHIP_BOARD_SIZE);
      const column = hit % BATTLESHIP_BOARD_SIZE;
      if (row > 0) adjacent.push(hit - BATTLESHIP_BOARD_SIZE);
      if (row < BATTLESHIP_BOARD_SIZE - 1) adjacent.push(hit + BATTLESHIP_BOARD_SIZE);
      if (column > 0) adjacent.push(hit - 1);
      if (column < BATTLESHIP_BOARD_SIZE - 1) adjacent.push(hit + 1);
    }
    const targets = [...new Set(adjacent)].filter(available);
    if (targets.length) return targets[Math.floor(this._random() * targets.length)];

    const hunt = Array.from({ length: CELL_COUNT }, (_, index) => index)
      .filter((index) => available(index));
    if (!hunt.length) return null;
    return hunt[Math.floor(this._random() * hunt.length)];
  }

  getSnapshot({ privateFor } = {}) {
    const isPrivate2P = this.state.mode === '2P';
    const players = {};
    for (const playerId of PLAYER_IDS) {
      const player = this.state.players[playerId];
      players[playerId] = { ready: player.ready };
      if (!isPrivate2P || (privateFor === playerId && privateFor === this.state.playerId)) {
        players[playerId].ships = player.ships.map((ship) => ({
          id: ship.id,
          length: ship.length,
          coordinates: [...ship.coordinates],
          orientation: ship.orientation,
          sunk: ship.sunk,
        }));
      }
    }
    return {
      version: 1,
      mode: this.state.mode,
      phase: this.state.phase,
      currentPlayerId: this.state.currentPlayerId,
      orientation: this.state.orientation,
      selectedShipId: this.state.selectedShipId,
      winner: this.state.winner,
      privateFor: privateFor === this.state.playerId ? privateFor : undefined,
      players,
      shots: {
        p1: this.state.shots.p1.map(copyShot),
        p2: this.state.shots.p2.map(copyShot),
      },
    };
  }

  loadSnapshot(snapshot, playerId = this.state.playerId) {
    if (!snapshot || snapshot.version !== 1 || !PLAYER_IDS.includes(playerId)) return false;
    if (!['1P', '2P'].includes(snapshot.mode) || !['setup', 'playing', 'finished'].includes(snapshot.phase)) return false;
    this.state.mode = snapshot.mode;
    this.state.phase = snapshot.phase;
    this.state.currentPlayerId = PLAYER_IDS.includes(snapshot.currentPlayerId) ? snapshot.currentPlayerId : 'p1';
    this.state.winner = PLAYER_IDS.includes(snapshot.winner) ? snapshot.winner : null;
    for (const id of PLAYER_IDS) {
      const incoming = snapshot.players?.[id];
      if (!incoming) continue;
      this.state.players[id].ready = incoming.ready === true;
      const mayLoadFleet = snapshot.mode === '1P'
        || (snapshot.privateFor === playerId && id === playerId);
      if (mayLoadFleet && Array.isArray(incoming.ships)) {
        for (const ship of this.state.players[id].ships) {
          const source = incoming.ships.find((item) => item.id === ship.id);
          if (!source || !Array.isArray(source.coordinates)) continue;
          if (source.coordinates.every((cell) => Number.isInteger(cell) && cell >= 0 && cell < CELL_COUNT)) {
            ship.coordinates = [...source.coordinates];
            ship.orientation = source.orientation === 'vertical' ? 'vertical' : 'horizontal';
            ship.sunk = source.sunk === true;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    for (const id of PLAYER_IDS) {
      if (Array.isArray(snapshot.shots?.[id])) {
        this.state.shots[id] = snapshot.shots[id]
          .filter((shot) => Number.isInteger(shot.index) && shot.index >= 0 && shot.index < CELL_COUNT
            && ['pending', 'hit', 'miss'].includes(shot.result))
          .map(copyShot);
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  _isFleetPlaced(playerId) {
    return this.state.players[playerId].ships.every((ship) => ship.coordinates.length === ship.length);
  }

  _random() {
    if (this._randomState === null) return Math.random();
    this._randomState = (1664525 * this._randomState + 1013904223) >>> 0;
    return this._randomState / 0x100000000;
  }

  _shuffle(items) {
    for (let index = items.length - 1; index > 0; index -= 1) {
      const other = Math.floor(this._random() * (index + 1));
      [items[index], items[other]] = [items[other], items[index]];
    }
  }
}

  '&': '&amp;',

  '>': '&gt;',
  '"': '&quot;',

})[character]);

export class BattleshipView {
  constructor({ root, t = (value) => value, onAction = () => {} } = {}) {
    if (!root) throw new TypeError('BattleshipView requires a root element');
    this.root = root;
    this.t = t;
    this.onAction = onAction;
    this.styleElement = document.createElement('style');
    this.styleElement.textContent = styles;
    this.root.append(this.styleElement);
    this.handleClick = this.handleClick.bind(this);
    this.handlePointerDown = this.handlePointerDown.bind(this);
    this.handlePointerUp = this.handlePointerUp.bind(this);
    this.handlePointerCancel = this.handlePointerCancel.bind(this);
    this.root.addEventListener('click', this.handleClick);
    this.root.addEventListener('pointerdown', this.handlePointerDown);
    this.root.addEventListener('pointerup', this.handlePointerUp);
    this.root.addEventListener('pointercancel', this.handlePointerCancel);
    this.drag = null;
  }

  render(snapshot, playerId) {
    const t = this.t;
    const mode = snapshot.mode;
    const phase = snapshot.phase;
    const ownId = playerId;
    const targetId = ownId === 'p1' ? 'p2' : 'p1';
    const own = snapshot.players[ownId] || { ships: [] };
    const ownShips = own.ships || [];
    const selected = snapshot.selectedShipId || 'carrier';
    const ownShots = snapshot.shots[ownId] || [];
    const incomingShots = snapshot.shots[targetId] || [];
    const allPlaced = ownShips.length === 5 && ownShips.every((ship) => ship.coordinates?.length === ship.length);
    let status;
    if (phase === 'finished') {
      status = snapshot.winner === ownId ? t('You win!') : t('Game over');
    } else if (phase === 'setup') {
      status = mode === '2P'
        ? (allPlaced ? t('Fleet ready. Waiting for the other player.') : t('Place your fleet on your board.'))
        : t('Place your fleet, then start the game.');
    } else if (mode === '2P') {
      status = snapshot.currentPlayerId === ownId ? t('Your turn. Choose a target cell.') : t('Opponentâ€™s turn.');
    } else {
      status = snapshot.currentPlayerId === 'p1' ? t('Your turn. Choose a target cell.') : t('Computer is choosing a target.');
    }

    const shipOptions = ownShips.map((ship) => {
      const placed = ship.coordinates?.length === ship.length;
      const shape = Array.from({ length: ship.length }, () => '<i></i>').join('');
      return `<button class="bs-ship-token${ship.id === selected ? ' is-selected' : ''}" type="button" data-action="select-ship" data-drag-ship="${escapeHtml(ship.id)}" aria-pressed="${ship.id === selected}" aria-label="${escapeHtml(shipName(ship.id, t))}, ${ship.length}${placed ? '' : `, ${escapeHtml(t('Not placed'))}`}"><span class="bs-ship-shape" aria-hidden="true">${shape}</span><span>${escapeHtml(shipName(ship.id, t))}${placed ? ' âœ“' : ''}</span></button>`;
    }).join('');
    const controls = phase === 'setup'
      ? `<div class="bs-setup-controls"><div class="bs-ship-picker" aria-label="${escapeHtml(t('Ship'))}">${shipOptions}</div><div class="bs-control-row"><button class="bs-button" type="button" data-action="rotate">${escapeHtml(t('Rotate'))} Â· ${escapeHtml(t(snapshot.orientation === 'vertical' ? 'vertical' : 'horizontal'))}</button><button class="bs-button" type="button" data-action="auto-place">${escapeHtml(t('Auto-place fleet'))}</button>${mode === '1P' ? `<button class="bs-button bs-button-primary" type="button" data-action="start" ${allPlaced ? '' : 'disabled'}>${escapeHtml(t('Start game'))}</button>` : ''}</div></div>`
      : '';
    const ownBoard = this.renderBoard({
      board: 'own', title: t(mode === '2P' ? 'Your fleet' : 'Your board'),
      ships: ownShips, shots: incomingShots, phase, mode, active: phase === 'setup', playerId: ownId,
    });
    const targetBoard = this.renderBoard({
      board: 'target', title: t(mode === '2P' ? 'Opponentâ€™s board' : 'Computerâ€™s board'),
      ships: [], shots: ownShots, phase, mode,
      active: phase === 'playing' && snapshot.currentPlayerId === ownId, playerId: ownId,
    });

    const boards = phase === 'setup'
      ? ownBoard
      : `${targetBoard}${ownBoard.replace('bs-board-panel', 'bs-board-panel bs-own-mini')}`;
    this.root.innerHTML = `<style>${styles}</style><section class="bs-game" data-phase="${phase}" aria-label="${escapeHtml(t('Battleship game'))}"><header class="bs-header"><h1>${escapeHtml(t('Battleship'))}</h1><div><button class="bs-button" type="button" data-action="exit">${escapeHtml(t('Exit'))}</button><button class="bs-button" type="button" data-action="restart">${escapeHtml(t('Restart'))}</button></div><span class="bs-mode">${escapeHtml(t(mode === '2P' ? '2P Â· Friends' : '1P Â· Solo'))}</span></header><p class="bs-status" role="status" aria-live="polite">${escapeHtml(status)}</p>${controls}<p class="bs-help">${escapeHtml(phase === 'setup' ? t('Drag a ship to your board. Tap a ship to select it; rotate before placing.') : t('Select a cell on the opposing board to fire.'))}</p><div class="bs-boards">${boards}</div><div class="bs-legend" aria-label="${escapeHtml(t('Board legend'))}"><span><i class="bs-mark"></i>${escapeHtml(t('Ship'))}</span><span><i class="bs-mark bs-mark-hit"></i>${escapeHtml(t('Hit'))}</span><span><i class="bs-mark bs-mark-miss"></i>${escapeHtml(t('Miss'))}</span><span><i class="bs-mark bs-mark-sunk"></i>${escapeHtml(t('Sunk'))}</span></div></section>`;
  }

  renderBoard({ board, title, ships, shots, phase, active, playerId }) {
    const t = this.t;
    const occupied = new Map();
    for (const ship of ships) {
      for (const index of ship.coordinates || []) occupied.set(index, ship);
    }
    const shotMap = new Map(shots.map((shot) => [shot.index, shot]));
    const sunkShipIds = new Set(shots.filter((shot) => shot.sunkShipId).map((shot) => shot.sunkShipId));
    let cells = '';
    for (let index = 0; index < SIZE * SIZE; index += 1) {
      const ship = occupied.get(index);
      const shot = shotMap.get(index);
      const classes = ['bs-cell'];
      let marker = '';
      if (ship?.sunk || (ship && sunkShipIds.has(ship.id))) {
        classes.push('bs-cell-sunk');
      } else if (ship) {
        classes.push('bs-cell-ship');
      }
      if (shot?.result === 'hit') {
        if (shot.sunkShipId || sunkShipIds.has(shot.shipId) || ship?.sunk) {
          classes.push('bs-cell-sunk');
        } else {
          classes.push('bs-cell-hit');
        }
        marker = shot.sunkShipId ? 'âœ•' : 'Ã—';
      } else if (shot?.result === 'miss') {
        classes.push('bs-cell-miss');
        marker = 'â€¢';
      }

      const disabled = !active || (board === 'target' && Boolean(shot)) || shot?.result === 'pending';
      const row = Math.floor(index / SIZE) + 1;
      const column = index % SIZE + 1;
      let description = `${t(board === 'own' ? 'Your board' : 'Opponent board')}, ${t('row')} ${row}, ${t('column')} ${column}`;
      if (ship) description += `, ${shipName(ship.id, t)}`;
      if (shot?.result === 'hit') description += `, ${t('Hit')}`;
      if (shot?.result === 'miss') description += `, ${t('Miss')}`;
      if (shot?.result === 'pending') description += `, ${t('Shot pending')}`;
      if (phase === 'setup' && board === 'own' && active) description += `, ${t('Place ship here')}`;
      if (board === 'target' && active && !shot) description += `, ${t('Fire here')}`;
      cells += `<button type="button" class="${classes.join(' ')}" data-action="cell" data-board="${board}" data-index="${index}" aria-label="${escapeHtml(description)}"${disabled ? ' disabled' : ''}>${marker}</button>`;
    }
    return `<section class="bs-board-panel" aria-label="${escapeHtml(title)}"><h2>${escapeHtml(title)}</h2><div class="bs-board" role="group" aria-label="${escapeHtml(title)}">${cells}</div></section>`;
  }

  handleClick(event) {
    const element = event.target.closest('[data-action]');
    if (!element || !this.root.contains(element)) return;
    if (element.dataset.action === 'select-ship') {
      this.onAction({ type: 'select-ship', shipId: element.dataset.dragShip });
      return;
    }
    if (element.dataset.action === 'cell') {
      this.onAction({ type: 'cell', board: element.dataset.board, index: Number(element.dataset.index) });
      return;
    }
    this.onAction({ type: element.dataset.action });
  }

  handlePointerDown(event) {
    const token = event.target.closest('[data-drag-ship]');
    if (!token || !this.root.contains(token)) return;
    this.drag = { shipId: token.dataset.dragShip, pointerId: event.pointerId, element: token };
    token.classList.add('is-dragging');
  }

  handlePointerUp(event) {
    if (!this.drag || event.pointerId !== this.drag.pointerId) return;
    const { shipId } = this.drag;
    this.drag.element.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    this.drag = null;
    const target = document.elementFromPoint(event.clientX, event.clientY)?.closest('[data-action="cell"][data-board="own"]');
    if (target && this.root.contains(target)) {
      this.onAction({ type: 'drop-ship', shipId, index: Number(target.dataset.index) });
    }
  }

  handlePointerCancel(event) {
    if (!this.drag || event.pointerId !== this.drag.pointerId) return;
    this.drag.element.classList.remove('is-dragging');
    this.drag = null;
  }

  dispose() {
    this.root.removeEventListener('click', this.handleClick);
    this.root.removeEventListener('pointerdown', this.handlePointerDown);
    this.root.removeEventListener('pointerup', this.handlePointerUp);
    this.root.removeEventListener('pointercancel', this.handlePointerCancel);
    this.root.replaceChildren();
  }
}
