const DOTS_COLUMNS = 4;
const DOTS_ROWS = 6;
const YAHTZEE_CATEGORIES = [
  { id: "ones", label: "Ones", section: "upper" },
  { id: "twos", label: "Twos", section: "upper" },
  { id: "threes", label: "Threes", section: "upper" },
  { id: "fours", label: "Fours", section: "upper" },
  { id: "fives", label: "Fives", section: "upper" },
  { id: "sixes", label: "Sixes", section: "upper" },
  { id: "threeKind", label: "Three of a kind", section: "lower" },
  { id: "fourKind", label: "Four of a kind", section: "lower" },
  { id: "fullHouse", label: "Full house", section: "lower" },
  { id: "smallStraight", label: "Small straight", section: "lower" },
  { id: "largeStraight", label: "Large straight", section: "lower" },
  { id: "yahtzee", label: "Yahtzee", section: "lower" },
  { id: "chance", label: "Chance", section: "lower" }
];

export class GameModel {
  constructor() {
    this.state = {
      room: "",
      gameType: "1a2b",
      playerId: "",
      playerName: "",
      mySecret: "",
      players: {},
      gameState: null,
      guesses: {},
      results: {},
      handledResults: new Set(),
      joined: false,
      joining: false,
      mode: "1P",
      soloMode: false,
      soloSecret: ""
    };
  }

  validName(name) {
    return name.trim().slice(0, 20);
  }

  isValidCode(value) {
    return /^(?!.*(.).*\1)[0-9]{4}$/.test(value);
  }

  isValidRoomCode(value) {
    return /^\d{6}$/.test(value);
  }

  scoreGuess(secret, guess) {
    const a = [...guess].filter((digit, index) => digit === secret[index]).length;
    const b = [...guess].filter((digit) => secret.includes(digit)).length - a;
    return { a, b };
  }

  createSecret() {
    const digits = "0123456789".split("");
    for (let index = digits.length - 1; index > 0; index--) {
      const swap = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(1))[0] % (index + 1);
      [digits[index], digits[swap]] = [digits[swap], digits[index]];
    }
    return digits.slice(0, 4).join("");
  }

  createRoomCode() {
    const value = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(1))[0] % 1000000;
    return String(value).padStart(6, "0");
  }

  getPlayers() {
    return Object.values(this.state.players)
      .filter((player) => player.online !== false)
      .sort((a, b) => (a.joinedAt || 0) - (b.joinedAt || 0) || a.id.localeCompare(b.id));
  }

  roomTopic() {
    const version = this.state.gameType === "dotsandboxes" ? "v2" : "v1";
    return `fourfold/${this.state.gameType}/${version}/${this.state.room}`;
  }

  playerTopic(id) {
    return `${this.roomTopic()}/players/${id}/presence`;
  }

  secretKey() {
    return `fourfold.secret.${this.state.room}.${this.state.playerId}`;
  }

  resetGame() {
    Object.assign(this.state, {
      room: "",
      gameType: "1a2b",
      playerId: "",
      players: {},
      gameState: null,
      guesses: {},
      results: {},
      handledResults: new Set(),
      joined: false,
      joining: false,
      soloMode: false,
      soloSecret: "",
      mySecret: ""
    });
  }

  createTicTacToeGame(players) {
    const [first, second] = players;
    return {
      phase: "playing",
      board: Array(9).fill(null),
      players: [
        { id: first.id, mark: "O" },
        { id: second.id, mark: "X" }
      ],
      activePlayerId: first.id,
      moveCount: 0,
      startedAt: Date.now()
    };
  }

  chooseTicTacToeMove(game, playerId) {
    const rootPlayerId = playerId;
    const path = new Set();
    const preferredCells = [4, 0, 2, 6, 8, 1, 3, 5, 7];

    const getPositionKey = (position) => {
      const age = new Map(
        position.board
          .map((move, cell) => move ? { cell, moveNumber: move.moveNumber } : null)
          .filter(Boolean)
          .sort((a, b) => a.moveNumber - b.moveNumber)
          .map((move, index) => [move.cell, index])
      );
      return `${position.activePlayerId}|${position.board.map((move, cell) =>
        move ? `${move.mark}${age.get(cell)}` : "-"
      ).join("")}`;
    };

    const search = (position, depth, alpha, beta) => {
      if (position.phase === "won") {
        return position.winnerId === rootPlayerId ? 100 - depth : depth - 100;
      }

      const key = getPositionKey(position);
      if (path.has(key)) return 0;
      path.add(key);

      const isMaximizing = position.activePlayerId === rootPlayerId;
      let bestScore = isMaximizing ? -Infinity : Infinity;
      const availableCells = preferredCells.filter((cell) => !position.board[cell]);
      for (const cell of availableCells) {
        const nextPosition = this.applyTicTacToeMove(position, position.activePlayerId, cell);
        if (!nextPosition) continue;
        const score = search(nextPosition, depth + 1, alpha, beta);
        if (isMaximizing) {
          bestScore = Math.max(bestScore, score);
          alpha = Math.max(alpha, bestScore);
        } else {
          bestScore = Math.min(bestScore, score);
          beta = Math.min(beta, bestScore);
        }
        if (beta <= alpha) break;
      }

      path.delete(key);
      return Number.isFinite(bestScore) ? bestScore : 0;
    };

    if (game?.phase !== "playing" || game.activePlayerId !== playerId) return null;
    let bestCell = null;
    let bestScore = -Infinity;
    let alpha = -Infinity;
    for (const cell of preferredCells.filter((index) => !game.board[index])) {
      const nextPosition = this.applyTicTacToeMove(game, playerId, cell);
      if (!nextPosition) continue;
      const score = search(nextPosition, 1, alpha, Infinity);
      if (score > bestScore) {
        bestScore = score;
        bestCell = cell;
      }
      alpha = Math.max(alpha, bestScore);
    }
    return bestCell;
  }

  applyTicTacToeMove(game, playerId, cell) {
    if (!game || game.phase !== "playing" || game.activePlayerId !== playerId ||
      !Number.isInteger(cell) || cell < 0 || cell > 8 || game.board[cell]) return null;

    const board = [...game.board];
    let removed = null;
    if (board.every(Boolean)) {
      removed = board.reduce((oldest, move, index) =>
        move.moveNumber < oldest.move.moveNumber ? { move, index } : oldest,
      { move: board[0], index: 0 });
      board[removed.index] = null;
    }

    const player = game.players.find((entry) => entry.id === playerId);
    if (!player) return null;
    const moveCount = game.moveCount + 1;
    board[cell] = { mark: player.mark, playerId, moveNumber: moveCount };
    const winnerId = this.getTicTacToeWinner(board);
    if (!winnerId && board.every(Boolean)) {
      removed = board.reduce((oldest, move, index) =>
        move.moveNumber < oldest.move.moveNumber ? { move, index } : oldest,
      { move: board[0], index: 0 });
      board[removed.index] = null;
    }
    const nextPlayer = game.players.find((entry) => entry.id !== playerId);

    return {
      ...game,
      board,
      moveCount,
      phase: winnerId ? "won" : "playing",
      winnerId,
      activePlayerId: winnerId ? null : nextPlayer.id,
      lastRemoved: removed ? { mark: removed.move.mark, cell: removed.index } : null,
      updatedAt: Date.now()
    };
  }

  getTicTacToeWinner(board) {
    const lines = [
      [0, 1, 2], [3, 4, 5], [6, 7, 8],
      [0, 3, 6], [1, 4, 7], [2, 5, 8],
      [0, 4, 8], [2, 4, 6]
    ];
    for (const [first, second, third] of lines) {
      const move = board[first];
      if (move && move.mark === board[second]?.mark && move.mark === board[third]?.mark) {
        return move.playerId;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  createDotsAndBoxesGame(players) {
    const [first, second] = players;
    const horizontalEdgeCount = (DOTS_ROWS + 1) * DOTS_COLUMNS;
    const edgeCount = horizontalEdgeCount + DOTS_ROWS * (DOTS_COLUMNS + 1);
    return {
      phase: "playing",
      columns: DOTS_COLUMNS,
      rows: DOTS_ROWS,
      edges: Array(edgeCount).fill(null),
      boxes: Array(DOTS_COLUMNS * DOTS_ROWS).fill(null),
      players: [
        { id: first.id, mark: "A" },
        { id: second.id, mark: "B" }
      ],
      scores: { [first.id]: 0, [second.id]: 0 },
      activePlayerId: first.id,
      moveCount: 0,
      startedAt: Date.now()
    };
  }

  getDotsBoxEdges(boxIndex) {
    const columns = DOTS_COLUMNS;
    const row = Math.floor(boxIndex / columns);
    const column = boxIndex % columns;
    const horizontalEdgeCount = (DOTS_ROWS + 1) * columns;
    return [
      row * columns + column,
      (row + 1) * columns + column,
      horizontalEdgeCount + row * (columns + 1) + column,
      horizontalEdgeCount + row * (columns + 1) + column + 1
    ];
  }

  isDotsAndBoxesGame(game) {
    return Boolean(game && game.columns === DOTS_COLUMNS && game.rows === DOTS_ROWS &&
      Array.isArray(game.edges) && game.edges.length === 58 &&
      Array.isArray(game.boxes) && game.boxes.length === 24);
  }

  applyDotsAndBoxesMove(game, playerId, edgeIndex) {
    if (!game || game.phase !== "playing" || game.activePlayerId !== playerId ||
      !Number.isInteger(edgeIndex) || edgeIndex < 0 || edgeIndex >= game.edges.length || game.edges[edgeIndex]) return null;
    const player = game.players.find((entry) => entry.id === playerId);
    if (!player) return null;

    const edges = [...game.edges];
    edges[edgeIndex] = { playerId, mark: player.mark, moveNumber: game.moveCount + 1 };
    const boxes = [...game.boxes];
    const completedBoxes = [];
    for (let boxIndex = 0; boxIndex < boxes.length; boxIndex++) {
      if (boxes[boxIndex]) continue;
      if (this.getDotsBoxEdges(boxIndex).every((edge) => edges[edge])) {
        boxes[boxIndex] = playerId;
        completedBoxes.push(boxIndex);
      }
    }

    const scores = { ...game.scores, [playerId]: game.scores[playerId] + completedBoxes.length };
    const moveCount = game.moveCount + 1;
    const finished = moveCount === game.edges.length;
    let winnerId = null;
    if (finished && scores[game.players[0].id] !== scores[game.players[1].id]) {
      winnerId = scores[game.players[0].id] > scores[game.players[1].id]
        ? game.players[0].id
        : game.players[1].id;
    }

    return {
      ...game,
      edges,
      boxes,
      scores,
      moveCount,
      phase: finished ? "won" : "playing",
      winnerId,
      activePlayerId: finished || completedBoxes.length ? (finished ? null : playerId) :
        game.players.find((entry) => entry.id !== playerId).id,
      lastMove: edgeIndex,
      lastCompletedBoxes: completedBoxes,
      updatedAt: Date.now()
    };
  }

  chooseDotsAndBoxesMove(game, playerId, nodeLimit = 30000) {
    if (game?.phase !== "playing" || game.activePlayerId !== playerId) return null;

    const boxEdges = Array.from({ length: game.boxes.length }, (_, box) =>
      this.getDotsBoxEdges(box)
    );
    const fullMask = (1n << BigInt(game.edges.length)) - 1n;
    let initialMask = 0n;
    game.edges.forEach((edge, index) => {
      if (edge) initialMask |= 1n << BigInt(index);
    });
    const transpositions = new Map();
    let nodes = 0;

    const sidesInBox = (mask, edges) =>
      edges.reduce((count, edge) => count + Number(Boolean(mask & (1n << BigInt(edge)))), 0);
    const movesFor = (mask) => {
      const captures = [];
      const safe = [];
      const all = [];
      for (let edge = 0; edge < game.edges.length; edge++) {
        const bit = 1n << BigInt(edge);
        if (mask & bit) continue;
        all.push(edge);
        let boxesCompleted = 0;
        let createsCapture = false;
        for (const edges of boxEdges) {
          if (edges.includes(edge)) {
            const sides = sidesInBox(mask, edges);
            if (sides === 3) createsCapture = true;
            if (sides === 3) boxesCompleted++;
          }
        }
        if (boxesCompleted) captures.push({ edge, boxesCompleted });
        if (!createsCapture) safe.push(edge);
      }
      const candidates = captures.length ? captures :
        safe.length ? safe.map((edge) => ({ edge, boxesCompleted: 0 })) :
          all.map((edge) => ({ edge, boxesCompleted: 0 }));
      candidates.sort((a, b) => b.boxesCompleted - a.boxesCompleted);
      return candidates;
    };
    const evaluate = (mask, turn) => {
      let value = 0;
      for (const edges of boxEdges) {
        const sides = sidesInBox(mask, edges);
        if (sides === 4) continue;
        if (sides === 3) value += turn === playerId ? 0.9 : -0.9;
        else if (sides === 2) value += turn === playerId ? 0.08 : -0.08;
      }
      return value;
    };

    const search = (mask, turn, depth, alpha, beta) => {
      nodes++;
      if (mask === fullMask) return 0;
      if (depth === 0 || nodes >= nodeLimit) return evaluate(mask, turn);

      const key = `${mask}:${turn}`;
      const cached = transpositions.get(key);
      if (cached !== undefined) return cached;

      const maximizing = turn === playerId;
      let best = maximizing ? -Infinity : Infinity;
      let fullySearched = true;
      for (const move of movesFor(mask)) {
        const nextMask = mask | (1n << BigInt(move.edge));
        const score = move.boxesCompleted * (maximizing ? 1 : -1) +
          search(nextMask, move.boxesCompleted ? turn : game.players.find((entry) => entry.id !== turn).id,
            depth - 1, alpha, beta);
        if (maximizing) {
          best = Math.max(best, score);
          alpha = Math.max(alpha, best);
        } else {
          best = Math.min(best, score);
          beta = Math.min(beta, best);
        }
        if (beta <= alpha || nodes >= nodeLimit) {
          fullySearched = false;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (fullySearched) transpositions.set(key, best);
      return Number.isFinite(best) ? best : 0;
    };

    let bestEdge = null;
    let bestScore = -Infinity;
    let alpha = -Infinity;
    for (const move of movesFor(initialMask)) {
      const turnAfterMove = move.boxesCompleted
        ? playerId
        : game.players.find((entry) => entry.id !== playerId).id;
      const score = move.boxesCompleted +
        search(initialMask | (1n << BigInt(move.edge)), turnAfterMove, 17, alpha, Infinity);
      if (score > bestScore) {
        bestScore = score;
        bestEdge = move.edge;
      }
      alpha = Math.max(alpha, bestScore);
      if (nodes >= nodeLimit) break;
    }
    return bestEdge;
  }

  createYahtzeeGame() {
    return {
      phase: "playing",
      round: 1,
      dice: Array(5).fill(0),
      held: Array(5).fill(false),
      rollsRemaining: 3,
      categories: Object.fromEntries(YAHTZEE_CATEGORIES.map(({ id }) => [id, null])),
      yahtzeeBonus: 0,
      startedAt: Date.now()
    };
  }

  createYahtzeeMatch(players) {
    if (!Array.isArray(players) || players.length !== 2 ||
      players.some((player) => !player?.id || !player.name)) return null;
    const game = this.createYahtzeeGame();
    return {
      ...game,
      players: players.map((player) => ({ id: player.id, name: player.name })),
      scorecards: Object.fromEntries(players.map((player) => [player.id, {
        categories: Object.fromEntries(YAHTZEE_CATEGORIES.map(({ id }) => [id, null])),
        yahtzeeBonus: 0,
        upperScore: 0,
        upperBonus: 0,
        totalScore: 0
      }])),
      activePlayerId: players[0]?.id || null,
      mode: "2P",
      moveCount: 0
    };
  }

  getYahtzeeCategories() {
    return YAHTZEE_CATEGORIES;
  }

  rollYahtzeeDice(game) {
    if (!game || game.phase !== "playing" || game.rollsRemaining <= 0) return null;
    const dice = game.dice.map((die, index) => game.held[index]
      ? die
      : crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(1))[0] % 6 + 1);
    return {
      ...game,
      dice,
      rollsRemaining: game.rollsRemaining - 1,
      moveCount: game.mode === "2P" ? (game.moveCount || 0) + 1 : game.moveCount,
      updatedAt: Date.now()
    };
  }

  toggleYahtzeeDie(game, dieIndex) {
    if (!game || game.phase !== "playing" || game.rollsRemaining <= 0 ||
      !Number.isInteger(dieIndex) || dieIndex < 0 || dieIndex >= game.dice.length ||
      !game.dice[dieIndex]) return null;
    const held = [...game.held];
    held[dieIndex] = !held[dieIndex];
    return {
      ...game,
      held,
      moveCount: game.mode === "2P" ? (game.moveCount || 0) + 1 : game.moveCount,
      updatedAt: Date.now()
    };
  }

  scoreYahtzeeCategory(dice, categoryId) {
    if (!Array.isArray(dice) || dice.length !== 5 || dice.some((die) => !Number.isInteger(die) || die < 1 || die > 6)) {
      return null;
    }
    const counts = Array(7).fill(0);
    for (const die of dice) counts[die]++;
    const total = dice.reduce((sum, die) => sum + die, 0);
    const unique = [...new Set(dice)].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    switch (categoryId) {
      case "ones":
      case "twos":
      case "threes":
      case "fours":
      case "fives":
      case "sixes": {
        const face = YAHTZEE_CATEGORIES.findIndex(({ id }) => id === categoryId) + 1;
        return counts[face] * face;
      }
      case "threeKind":
        return counts.some((count) => count >= 3) ? total : 0;
      case "fourKind":
        return counts.some((count) => count >= 4) ? total : 0;
      case "fullHouse":
        return counts.includes(3) && counts.includes(2) ? 25 : 0;
      case "smallStraight":
        return ["1,2,3,4", "2,3,4,5", "3,4,5,6"]
          .some((sequence) => sequence.split(",").every((face) => unique.includes(Number(face)))) ? 30 : 0;
      case "largeStraight":
        return unique.length === 5 && (unique[0] === 1 && unique[4] === 5 || unique[0] === 2 && unique[4] === 6)
          ? 40
          : 0;
      case "yahtzee":
        return counts.some((count) => count === 5) ? 50 : 0;
      case "chance":
        return total;
      default:
        return null;
    }
  }

  getYahtzeeTurnScore(game, categoryId) {
    const card = game?.mode === "2P" ? game.scorecards?.[game.activePlayerId] : game;
    if (!game || !card || game.phase !== "playing" || game.dice.some((die) => !die) ||
      !Object.hasOwn(card.categories, categoryId) || card.categories[categoryId] !== null) return null;

    const category = YAHTZEE_CATEGORIES.find(({ id }) => id === categoryId);
    const isYahtzee = game.dice.every((die) => die === game.dice[0]);
    const hasJoker = isYahtzee && card.categories.yahtzee === 50;
    if (!hasJoker) return this.scoreYahtzeeCategory(game.dice, categoryId);

    const forcedUpperCategory = ["ones", "twos", "threes", "fours", "fives", "sixes"][game.dice[0] - 1];
    if (card.categories[forcedUpperCategory] === null) {
      return categoryId === forcedUpperCategory ? game.dice[0] * 5 : null;
    }

    const hasOpenLowerCategory = YAHTZEE_CATEGORIES.some(({ id, section }) =>
      section === "lower" && card.categories[id] === null
    );
    if (category.section === "upper") return hasOpenLowerCategory ? null : 0;
    if (categoryId === "fullHouse") return 25;
    if (categoryId === "smallStraight") return 30;
    if (categoryId === "largeStraight") return 40;
    return this.scoreYahtzeeCategory(game.dice, categoryId);
  }

  scoreYahtzeeTurn(game, categoryId) {
    const score = this.getYahtzeeTurnScore(game, categoryId);
    if (score === null) return null;
    const multiPlayer = game.mode === "2P";
    const card = multiPlayer ? game.scorecards[game.activePlayerId] : game;
    const isBonusYahtzee = game.dice.every((die) => die === game.dice[0]) && card.categories.yahtzee === 50;
    const categories = { ...card.categories, [categoryId]: score };
    const round = Object.values(categories).filter((value) => value !== null).length + 1;
    const cardComplete = round > YAHTZEE_CATEGORIES.length;
    const upperScore = YAHTZEE_CATEGORIES
      .filter(({ section }) => section === "upper")
      .reduce((sum, { id }) => sum + (categories[id] || 0), 0);
    const upperBonus = upperScore >= 63 ? 35 : 0;
    const yahtzeeBonus = (card.yahtzeeBonus || 0) + (isBonusYahtzee ? 100 : 0);
    const totalScore = Object.values(categories).reduce((sum, value) => sum + (value || 0), 0) +
      upperBonus + yahtzeeBonus;
    if (multiPlayer) {
      const scorecards = {
        ...game.scorecards,
        [game.activePlayerId]: { categories, upperScore, upperBonus, yahtzeeBonus, totalScore }
      };
      const matchComplete = Object.values(scorecards).every((scorecard) =>
        Object.values(scorecard.categories).every((value) => value !== null)
      );
      const firstTotal = scorecards[game.players[0].id].totalScore;
      const secondTotal = scorecards[game.players[1].id].totalScore;
      const nextPlayer = game.players.find((player) => player.id !== game.activePlayerId);
      const activePlayerId = matchComplete ? null : nextPlayer.id;
      const nextRound = matchComplete
        ? YAHTZEE_CATEGORIES.length
        : Object.values(scorecards[activePlayerId].categories).filter((value) => value !== null).length + 1;
      return {
        ...game,
        phase: matchComplete ? "won" : "playing",
        round: nextRound,
        dice: Array(5).fill(0),
        held: Array(5).fill(false),
        rollsRemaining: 3,
        scorecards,
        activePlayerId,
        winnerId: matchComplete
          ? firstTotal === secondTotal ? null : firstTotal > secondTotal ? game.players[0].id : game.players[1].id
          : null,
        moveCount: (game.moveCount || 0) + 1,
        startedAt: game.startedAt,
        updatedAt: Date.now()
      };
    }
    return {
      ...game,
      phase: cardComplete ? "won" : "playing",
      round,
      dice: Array(5).fill(0),
      held: Array(5).fill(false),
      rollsRemaining: 3,
      categories,
      upperScore,
      upperBonus,
      yahtzeeBonus,
      totalScore,
      updatedAt: Date.now()
    };
  }
}

  renderTicTacToe(state, getPlayers, pendingMove = false) {
    const game = state.gameState;
    const players = game?.players || [];
    const activePlayers = getPlayers();
    const markFor = (playerId) => players.find((player) => player.id === playerId)?.mark;
    const playerName = (playerId) => state.players[playerId]?.name || "Player";
    this.elements.ticTacToeRoomCode.textContent = state.room;
    this.elements.ticTacToePlayers.textContent = players.length
      ? players.map((player) => `${playerName(player.id)} Â· ${player.mark}`).join("   ")
      : activePlayers.map((player) => player.name).join("   ");

    if (!game) {
      this.elements.ticTacToeStatus.textContent = "Waiting for a player to join your room.";
    } else if (game.phase === "won") {
      this.elements.ticTacToeStatus.textContent = `${playerName(game.winnerId)} (${markFor(game.winnerId)}) wins!`;
    } else if (activePlayers.length !== 2 && game.phase === "playing") {
      this.elements.ticTacToeStatus.textContent = "Waiting for a player to reconnect.";
    } else {
      const activeName = playerName(game.activePlayerId);
      const activeMark = markFor(game.activePlayerId);
      const turnMessage = game.activePlayerId === state.playerId
        ? `Your turn â€” you are ${activeMark}.`
        : `${activeName} (${activeMark}) is choosing.`;
      this.elements.ticTacToeStatus.textContent = game.lastRemoved
        ? `Oldest ${game.lastRemoved.mark} removed. ${turnMessage}`
        : turnMessage;
    }

    this.elements.ticTacToeBoard.replaceChildren(...Array.from({ length: 9 }, (_, index) => {
      const move = game?.board[index];
      const cell = this.document.createElement("button");
      cell.type = "button";
      cell.className = "tic-tac-toe-cell";
      cell.dataset.cell = String(index);
      cell.dataset.mark = move?.mark || "";
      cell.textContent = move?.mark || "";
      cell.classList.toggle("is-oldest", game?.lastRemoved?.cell === index);
      cell.setAttribute("aria-label", `Square ${index + 1}${move ? `, ${move.mark}` : ", empty"}`);
      cell.disabled = !game || game.phase !== "playing" || game.activePlayerId !== state.playerId ||
        activePlayers.length !== 2 || pendingMove || Boolean(move);
      return cell;
    }));
  }

  renderDotsAndBoxes(state, getPlayers, pendingMove = false, localMode = false) {
    const game = state.gameState;
    const players = game?.players || [];
    const activePlayers = getPlayers();
    const playerName = (playerId) => state.players[playerId]?.name || "Player";
    const markFor = (playerId) => players.find((player) => player.id === playerId)?.mark || "";
    this.elements.dotsRoomCode.textContent = state.room || "LOCAL";
    this.elements.dotsPlayers.textContent = players
      .map((player) => `${playerName(player.id)} Â· ${player.mark}`)
      .join("   ");

    if (!game) {
      this.elements.dotsStatus.textContent = "Waiting for a player to join your room.";
    } else if (game.phase === "won") {
      if (game.winnerId) {
        const loser = players.find((player) => player.id !== game.winnerId);
        this.elements.dotsStatus.textContent =
          `${playerName(game.winnerId)} wins ${game.scores[game.winnerId]}â€“${game.scores[loser?.id]}!`;
      } else {
        this.elements.dotsStatus.textContent = `It's a tie! ${game.scores[players[0]?.id]}â€“${game.scores[players[1]?.id]}.`;
      }
    } else if (activePlayers.length !== 2 && !localMode) {
      this.elements.dotsStatus.textContent = "Waiting for a player to reconnect.";
    } else if (pendingMove && localMode && game.activePlayerId !== state.playerId) {
      this.elements.dotsStatus.textContent = "Computer is thinkingâ€¦";
    } else {
      this.elements.dotsStatus.textContent = game.activePlayerId === state.playerId
        ? "Your turn â€” draw a line."
        : `${playerName(game.activePlayerId)}'s turn.`;
    }

    this.elements.dotsScores.replaceChildren(...players.map((player) => {
      const score = this.document.createElement("div");
      score.className = "dots-score";
      score.dataset.mark = player.mark;
      score.classList.toggle("is-active", game?.phase === "playing" && game.activePlayerId === player.id);
      const name = this.document.createElement("span");
      name.textContent = playerName(player.id);
      const value = this.document.createElement("strong");
      value.textContent = String(game?.scores[player.id] || 0);
      score.append(name, value);
      return score;
    }));

    const rows = game?.rows || 6;
    const columns = game?.columns || 4;
    const horizontalEdgeCount = (rows + 1) * columns;
    const cells = [];
    for (let row = 0; row < rows * 2 + 1; row++) {
      for (let column = 0; column < columns * 2 + 1; column++) {
        if (row % 2 === 0 && column % 2 === 0) {
          const dot = this.document.createElement("span");
          dot.className = "dots-node";
          cells.push(dot);
        } else if (row % 2 === 0) {
          const edge = (row / 2) * columns + Math.floor(column / 2);
          cells.push(this.createDotsEdge(edge, "horizontal", game, state, activePlayers, pendingMove, localMode));
        } else if (column % 2 === 0) {
          const edge = horizontalEdgeCount + Math.floor(row / 2) * (columns + 1) + column / 2;
          cells.push(this.createDotsEdge(edge, "vertical", game, state, activePlayers, pendingMove, localMode));
        } else {
          const box = Math.floor(row / 2) * columns + Math.floor(column / 2);
          const square = this.document.createElement("span");
          square.className = "dots-box";
          const owner = game?.boxes[box];
          square.dataset.mark = markFor(owner);
          square.textContent = owner ? markFor(owner) : "";
          cells.push(square);
        }
      }
    }
    this.elements.dotsBoard.replaceChildren(...cells);
  }

  createDotsEdge(edge, orientation, game, state, players, pendingMove, localMode) {
    const move = game?.edges[edge];
    const button = this.document.createElement("button");
    button.type = "button";
    button.className = `dots-edge is-${orientation}`;
    button.dataset.edge = String(edge);
    button.dataset.mark = move?.mark || "";
    button.setAttribute("aria-label", `Draw ${orientation} line ${edge + 1}${move ? `, claimed by ${move.mark}` : ""}`);
    button.disabled = !game || game.phase !== "playing" || game.activePlayerId !== state.playerId ||
      players.length !== 2 && !localMode || pendingMove || Boolean(move);
    return button;
  }

  renderYahtzee(state, model, pendingMove = false) {
    const game = state.gameState;
    if (!game) {
      this.elements.yahtzeeModeBadge.textContent = "2P Â· Friends";
      this.elements.yahtzeeHint.textContent = "Waiting for your friend to join.";
      this.elements.yahtzeeRoll.disabled = true;
      this.elements.yahtzeeRestart.disabled = true;
      this.elements.yahtzeeDice.replaceChildren();
      this.elements.yahtzeeScoreList.replaceChildren();
      this.elements.yahtzeeMatchScores.replaceChildren();
      this.elements.yahtzeeMatchScores.hidden = true;
      return;
    }
    const categories = model.getYahtzeeCategories();
    const multiPlayer = game.mode === "2P";
    const displayedPlayerId = multiPlayer
      ? game.activePlayerId || state.playerId
      : null;
    const card = multiPlayer ? game.scorecards[displayedPlayerId] : game;
    const isMyTurn = !multiPlayer || game.activePlayerId === state.playerId;
    const hasRolled = game.dice.some(Boolean);
    const heldCount = game.held.filter(Boolean).length;
    this.elements.yahtzeeTotal.textContent = String(card?.totalScore || 0);
    this.elements.yahtzeeRound.textContent = this.translate("{round} of 13", {
      round: String(Math.min(game.round, 13)).padStart(2, "0")
    });
    this.elements.yahtzeeModeBadge.textContent = multiPlayer ? "2P Â· Friends" : "1P Â· Solo";
    this.elements.yahtzeeMatchScores.hidden = !multiPlayer;
    if (multiPlayer) {
      this.elements.yahtzeeMatchScores.replaceChildren(...game.players.map((player) => {
        const score = this.document.createElement("span");
        score.className = "yahtzee-player-score";
        score.classList.toggle("is-turn", player.id === game.activePlayerId && game.phase === "playing");
        const name = this.document.createElement("strong");
        name.textContent = player.id === state.playerId ? `${player.name} (you)` : player.name;
        const total = this.document.createElement("b");
        total.textContent = String(game.scorecards[player.id].totalScore);
        score.append(name, total);
        return score;
      }));
    } else {
      this.elements.yahtzeeMatchScores.replaceChildren();
    }
    this.elements.yahtzeeRollLabel.textContent = hasRolled
      ? this.translate("ROLL {roll} OF 3", { roll: 3 - game.rollsRemaining })
      : this.translate("READY TO ROLL");
    this.elements.yahtzeeRoll.textContent = game.phase === "won"
      ? multiPlayer
        ? game.winnerId
          ? this.translate("{name} wins", { name: game.players.find(({ id }) => id === game.winnerId)?.name })
          : this.translate("Itâ€™s a tie")
        : this.translate("Game complete")
      : multiPlayer && !isMyTurn
        ? this.translate("Waiting for your turn")
        : game.rollsRemaining === 3
          ? this.translate("Roll dice Â· 3 left")
          : this.translate("Roll again Â· {count} left", { count: game.rollsRemaining });
    this.elements.yahtzeeRoll.disabled = game.phase !== "playing" || game.rollsRemaining === 0 ||
      !isMyTurn || pendingMove;
    this.elements.yahtzeeHeldCount.textContent = `${heldCount} held`;
    this.elements.yahtzeeHint.textContent = game.phase === "won"
      ? multiPlayer
        ? game.winnerId
          ? this.translate("{name} wins with {score} points.", {
            name: game.players.find(({ id }) => id === game.winnerId)?.name,
            score: game.scorecards[game.winnerId].totalScore
          })
          : this.translate("Both players finished with {score} points.", {
            score: game.scorecards[game.players[0].id].totalScore
          })
        : `Game complete â€” you scored ${game.totalScore} points.`
      : multiPlayer && !isMyTurn
        ? this.translate("{name} is playing.", {
          name: game.players.find(({ id }) => id === game.activePlayerId)?.name || this.translate("Your opponent")
        })
      : !hasRolled
        ? "Tap Roll to start your turn."
        : game.rollsRemaining > 0
          ? "Tap dice to hold them, then roll again."
          : "Choose a category to score this turn.";

    const pipSlots = {
      1: [5],
      2: [1, 9],
      3: [1, 5, 9],
      4: [1, 3, 7, 9],
      5: [1, 3, 5, 7, 9],
      6: [1, 3, 4, 6, 7, 9]
    };
    this.elements.yahtzeeDice.replaceChildren(...game.dice.map((die, index) => {
      const button = this.document.createElement("button");
      button.type = "button";
      button.className = "yahtzee-die";
      button.dataset.die = String(index);
      button.classList.toggle("is-empty", !die);
      button.classList.toggle("is-held", game.held[index]);
      button.setAttribute("aria-label", die
        ? `Die ${index + 1}: ${die}${game.held[index] ? ", held" : ""}`
        : `Die ${index + 1}: not rolled`);
      button.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(game.held[index]));
      button.disabled = !die || game.rollsRemaining === 0 || game.phase !== "playing" ||
        !isMyTurn || pendingMove;
      for (let slot = 1; slot <= 9; slot++) {
        const pip = this.document.createElement("span");
        pip.className = "die-pip";
        if (pipSlots[die]?.includes(slot)) pip.classList.add("is-visible");
        button.append(pip);
      }
      return button;
    }));

    this.elements.yahtzeeScoreList.replaceChildren(...categories.map((category) => {
      const score = card?.categories[category.id];
      const button = this.document.createElement("button");
      button.type = "button";
      button.className = "yahtzee-score-row";
      button.dataset.category = category.id;
      button.classList.toggle("is-scored", score !== null);
      const preview = score === null ? model.getYahtzeeTurnScore(game, category.id) : score;
      button.disabled = game.phase !== "playing" || !isMyTurn || pendingMove ||
        preview === null || score !== null;
      const label = this.document.createElement("span");
      label.textContent = this.translate(category.label);
      const value = this.document.createElement("strong");
      value.textContent = score !== null
        ? String(score)
        : hasRolled && preview !== null ? String(preview) : "â€”";
      button.append(label, value);
      return button;
    }));
    const categoriesLeft = 13 - Object.values(card?.categories || {}).filter((value) => value !== null).length;
    this.elements.yahtzeeScorecardFooter.textContent = this.translate(
      "Upper {score} / 63{bonus}{yahtzee} Â· {count} categories left",
      {
        score: card?.upperScore || 0,
        bonus: card?.upperBonus ? this.translate("Â· +35 bonus") : "",
        yahtzee: card?.yahtzeeBonus
          ? this.translate("Â· Yahtzee +{score}", { score: card.yahtzeeBonus })
          : "",
        count: categoriesLeft
      }
    );
    this.elements.yahtzeeRestart.disabled = pendingMove;
  }

  renderPlayers(state, getPlayers) {
    const activePlayers = getPlayers();
    const displayPlayers = [...activePlayers];
    if (!displayPlayers.some((player) => player.id === state.playerId) && state.joined) {
      displayPlayers.unshift(state.players[state.playerId] || {
        id: state.playerId,
        name: state.playerName,
        online: true,
        ready: false
      });
    }

    this.elements.players.replaceChildren(...[displayPlayers[0], displayPlayers[1]].map((player, index) => {
      const card = this.document.createElement("div");
      card.className = "player-card";
      if (state.gameState?.phase === "playing" && player?.id === state.gameState.activePlayerId) {
        card.classList.add("is-turn");
      }
      if (!player) {
        card.innerHTML = `<span class="player-avatar">Â·Â·</span><span class="waiting-slot">Waiting for player ${index + 1}â€¦</span>`;
        return card;
      }
      const avatar = this.document.createElement("span");
      avatar.className = "player-avatar";
      avatar.textContent = player.name.slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();
      const details = this.document.createElement("span");
      details.className = "player-details";
      const name = this.document.createElement("span");
      name.className = "player-name";
      name.textContent = player.id === state.playerId ? `${player.name} (you)` : player.name;
      const status = this.document.createElement("span");
      status.className = "player-status";
      status.textContent = player.online === false ? "Disconnected" : player.ready ? "Secret set Â· Ready" : "Choosing a secret";
      details.append(name, status);
      const playerState = this.document.createElement("span");
      playerState.className = "player-state";
      playerState.textContent = player.ready ? "READY" : player.online === false ? "OFFLINE" : "ONLINE";
      card.append(avatar, details, playerState);
      return card;
    }));
  }

  render(state, getPlayers, onCurrentResult) {
    this.renderPlayers(state, getPlayers);
    if (state.soloMode) {
      this.renderSoloGame(state);
      return;
    }

    const activePlayers = getPlayers();
    const me = state.players[state.playerId];
    const opponent = activePlayers.find((player) => player.id !== state.playerId);
    if (!state.gameState) {
      this.elements.readyStatus.textContent = me?.ready ? "You are ready" : "Not ready yet";
      this.elements.secretSubmit.textContent = me?.ready ? "Update secret" : "Lock in secret";
      this.elements.secretSubmit.disabled = false;
      this.elements.secret.disabled = false;
      this.elements.secret.value = state.mySecret;
      this.elements.guessForm.hidden = true;
      this.elements.turnTitle.textContent = "Waiting for both players";
      this.elements.turnHint.textContent = opponent
        ? `${opponent.name} needs to set a secret too.`
        : "Share the room code to invite your opponent.";
      this.elements.turnNumber.textContent = "â€”";
    } else if (state.gameState.phase === "won") {
      this.elements.secretSubmit.disabled = true;
      this.elements.secret.disabled = true;
      this.elements.readyStatus.textContent = "ROUND COMPLETE";
      const winner = state.players[state.gameState.winnerId]?.name || "A player";
      this.elements.turnTitle.textContent = state.gameState.winnerId === state.playerId
        ? "You cracked the code!"
        : `${winner} cracked your code`;
      this.elements.turnHint.textContent = "A perfect 4A ends the game.";
      this.elements.turnNumber.textContent = String(Math.max(1, state.gameState.turn));
      this.elements.guessForm.hidden = true;
      this.setBanner(state.gameState.winnerId === state.playerId
        ? "You win â€” all four digits, in the right order!"
        : `${winner} wins this round.`, "neutral");
    } else {
      this.elements.secretSubmit.disabled = true;
      this.elements.secret.disabled = true;
      this.elements.readyStatus.textContent = "GAME IN PROGRESS";
      const isMyTurn = state.gameState.activePlayerId === state.playerId;
      const whoseTurn = state.players[state.gameState.activePlayerId]?.name || "Opponent";
      this.elements.turnTitle.textContent = isMyTurn ? "Your turn to guess" : `${whoseTurn} is guessing`;
      this.elements.turnHint.textContent = isMyTurn
        ? `Try to crack ${opponent?.name || "your opponent"}â€™s secret.`
        : "Your opponent is trying to crack your secret.";
      this.elements.turnNumber.textContent = String(state.gameState.turn);
      this.elements.guessForm.hidden = !isMyTurn;
      this.elements.guessSubmit.disabled = !isMyTurn || Boolean(state.guesses[state.gameState.turn]) || !state.mySecret;
      if (isMyTurn && !state.mySecret) this.setBanner("Re-enter your secret number below before taking your turn.", "error");
      else if (!state.guesses[state.gameState.turn] && isMyTurn) this.setBanner("Your turn. Enter four different digits to make a guess.");
      else if (!isMyTurn) this.setBanner("Waiting for your opponentâ€™s guess.");
    }
    this.updateGuessSlots(state);
    this.renderHistory(state);
    onCurrentResult();
  }

  renderSoloGame(state) {
    this.elements.turnNumber.textContent = String(state.gameState.turn);
    this.elements.guessForm.hidden = state.gameState.phase === "won";
    this.elements.guessSubmit.disabled = state.gameState.phase === "won";
    this.elements.soloSecret.hidden = false;
    this.updateGuessSlots(state);
    if (state.gameState.phase === "won") {
      this.elements.turnTitle.textContent = "You got it!";
      this.elements.turnHint.textContent = "";
      this.setBanner(`The code was ${state.soloSecret}.`);
    } else {
      this.elements.turnTitle.textContent = "Guess the code";
      this.elements.turnHint.textContent = "";
      this.setBanner("Find the secret. Every clue counts.");
    }
    this.renderHistory(state);
  }

  renderHistory(state) {
    const turns = Object.keys(state.guesses).map(Number)
      .filter((turn) => state.guesses[turn].by === state.playerId)
      .sort((a, b) => a - b);
    if (!turns.length) {
      const empty = this.document.createElement("div");
      empty.className = "empty-history";
      empty.textContent = "Guesses will appear here once the game begins.";
      this.elements.history.replaceChildren(empty);
      return;
    }
    this.elements.history.replaceChildren(...turns.map((turn) => {
      const guess = state.guesses[turn];
      const item = this.document.createElement("div");
      item.className = "history-item";
      const index = this.document.createElement("span");
      index.className = "history-index";
      index.textContent = String(turn).padStart(2, "0");
      const detail = this.document.createElement("span");
      const player = this.document.createElement("span");
      player.className = "history-player";
      const name = this.document.createElement("strong");
      name.textContent = state.soloMode
        ? state.playerName
        : (state.players[guess.by]?.name || (guess.by === state.playerId ? state.playerName : "Opponent"));
      player.append(name, this.document.createTextNode(guess.by === state.playerId ? " Â· guessed" : " guessed"));
      const digits = this.document.createElement("span");
      digits.className = "history-guess";
      digits.textContent = guess.guess;
      detail.append(player, digits);
      const outcome = this.document.createElement("span");
      outcome.className = "history-result";
      if (state.results[turn]) {
        const bulls = this.document.createElement("span");
        bulls.textContent = `${state.results[turn].a}A`;
        const cows = this.document.createElement("span");
        cows.textContent = `${state.results[turn].b}B`;
        outcome.append(bulls, cows);
      } else {
        outcome.textContent = "â€¦";
      }
      item.append(index, detail, outcome);
      return item;
    }).reverse());
  }

  updateGuessSlots(state) {
    const value = this.elements.guess.value;
    [...this.elements.guessSlots.children].forEach((slot, index) => {
      slot.textContent = value[index] || (index === value.length ? "âˆ’" : "");
      slot.classList.toggle("is-empty", index === value.length);
    });
    this.elements.keypad.querySelectorAll("[data-digit]").forEach((button) => {
      const used = value.includes(button.dataset.digit);
      button.disabled = used;
      button.classList.toggle("is-used", used);
      button.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(used));
    });
    this.elements.keypadSubmit.disabled = value.length !== 4 || new Set(value).size !== value.length ||
      (state.soloMode && state.gameState?.phase === "won") ||
      (!state.soloMode && (!state.gameState || state.gameState.phase !== "playing" ||
        state.gameState.activePlayerId !== state.playerId || Boolean(state.guesses[state.gameState.turn]) || !state.mySecret));
    this.elements.keypadSubmit.textContent = value.length === 4 ? "Submit guess" : "Add one more digit to submit";
    const count = this.document.querySelector("#guess-count");
    if (count) count.textContent = `GUESS ${String(Object.keys(state.guesses).length + 1).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  }
}
